Abstract: In four-flavor massless QED with a magnetic monopole, a single incident fermion is scattered into a state carrying a half-integral current vector in each flavor channel, called the semiton. Recently, the semiton has been interpreted as a twisted-sector fermion, but its microscopic Hilbert-space representation has not been fully developed. In this Letter, using a bosonized fermion--rotor model, we construct the semiton state explicitly. This is achieved by first identifying the correct vacuum of the system, and then constructing a unitary operator that shifts the rotor and creates a particle-hole cloud in each flavor channel. The semiton state is obtained by applying this operator to an ordinary fermion state. The resulting state has a localized fermion flavor density in the core region and a half-integral semiton front at the wavepacket. We also show that the state carries the correct $U(1)_M$ charge expectation values and energy-density profile. Our result provides a concrete Hilbert-space realization of the varying-Fock-space interpretation of monopole--fermion scattering, advocated in previous works by the author and collaborators.

Abstract: We investigate the Page curve and information recovery in a near-extremal regular black hole inspired by T-duality, in which the central singularity is resolved by a minimal length scale. Consequently, the extremal remnant carries a finite entropy of purely quantum origin. Although both prescriptions produce a Page transition, they predict different saturation values and Page times. While the standard functional yields a plateau controlled by the area term, the corrected prescription saturates at the full thermodynamic entropy of the outer horizon and, in the extremal limit, at the logarithmic remnant entropy. Including evaporation and backreaction, the Page curve develops the expected descending branch and asymptotes to the entropy of the cold extremal remnant rather than to zero. Our results indicate that a thermodynamically consistent description of information recovery from regular near-extremal black holes requires incorporating the intrinsic quantum correction to the gravitational entropy.

For monochromatic two-color noncommuting plane waves, explicit weak-field expressions are derived for the induced Pauli coefficient and the anomalous spin-precession frequency. Abstract: We study the quantum dynamics of supersymmetric Galilean Yang-Mills (SGYM), with a focus on three dimensions where there is a classical scale symmetry. Unlike the relativistic case, we find that SGYM only admits perturbative dynamical excitations on the Coulomb branch. Conclusion: 该结果对全息原理（holography）和黑洞信息问题（black hole information problem）具有直接启示：渐近引力场的量子态可携带紧致源的完全量子信息，且黑洞视界外的引力状态与黑洞内部态相关联，为理解黑洞信息是否丢失提供了新的理论视角。

Abstract: We derive the quantum state of the asymptotic linearized gravity field of a compact energy source, using BRST quantization. Conclusion: 论文确立了一项关于$\mathcal{N}=4$超杨-米尔斯理论非可逆对称性与顶点算子代数外自同构之间的对应关系，并通过多项检验支持这一提议。

Abstract: We argue that the non-invertible symmetry of $\mathcal{N}=4$ super Yang-Mills theory, obtained by combining S-duality, half-space gauging, and R-symmetry twist, is realized as an outer-automorphism of the associated vertex operator algebra. Hence, the Schur and Macdonald index of $\mathcal{N}=4$ super Yang-Mills theory with non-invertible symmetry twist corresponds to the (refined) vacuum character of the associated vertex operator algebra with the outer-automorphism twist. We provide a few checks of our proposal from the large-$N$ limit and by matching the fusion rules, which involve charge conjugation on local operators. The `charged' Schur index indeed matches the outer-automorphism-twisted vacuum character.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究带有中性标量毛的 AdS 黑洞解及其全息性质，旨在通过微扰方法在超引力截断中构建新的黑洞族，并考察其热力学和输运性质偏离最大超对称结果的方式。

Method: 在 G2 不变截断的四维双荷 N=8 ISO(7) 规范超引力中，围绕 G2 对称 AdS4-Schwarzschild 黑洞，以无量纲标量荷参数 ε 为展开参数进行微扰展开。将微扰层级逐阶化为嵌套的非齐次 Legendre 方程，并利用 Pöschl-Teller 方程和 Fefferman-Graham 展开求解到二阶。

Result: 一阶标量场满足参数 ν(ν+1)=2/3 的 Pöschl-Teller 方程，一阶度规由同一函数代数确定；二阶回馈给出了重整化自由能、熵密度在固定温度下的修正，以及在固定熵或能量密度下将 Hawking 温度降低 δT/T=O(ε²)。剪切粘度维持 η/s=1/(4π)，而体粘度 ζ/η≈0.471ε²，声速平方小于 1/2，表明状态方程变软。

Conclusion: 该微扰黑洞解满足第一定律，其输运系数显示出标量毛对全息流体性质的修正：剪切粘度保持通用下限而体粘度非零，且声速降低表明状态方程软化，相关结果通过 Fefferman-Graham 非线性映射与 AdS/CFT 字典相衔接。

Abstract: We construct perturbative black-brane solutions carrying neutral scalar hair in the $G_{2}$-invariant truncation of four-dimensional dyonic $\mathcal{N}=8$ ISO(7) gauged supergravity, an exact consistent truncation of massive type~IIA supergravity on $S^{6}$. Expanding around the $G_{2}$-symmetric AdS$_{4}$-Schwarzschild black brane in powers of a dimensionless scalar-charge parameter $\eps$, we show that the perturbation hierarchy reduces, order by order, to a nested sequence of inhomogeneous Legendre equations (demonstrated through second order). The first-order scalar satisfies a Pöschl--Teller equation with exact parameter $ν(ν+1)=2/3$, giving $ν=-\frac{1}{2}+\frac{\sqrt{33}}{6}$, and the first-order metric is fixed algebraically by the same function, $A^{(1)}=-\tfrac{\sqrt{7}}{2}φ^{(1)}$, so the entire $\mathcal{O}(\eps)$ sector is one Legendre function. An identity $ν+1=Δ_{+}/3$, with $Δ_{+}=(3+\sqrt{33})/2\approx 4.372$ the conformal dimension of the dual scalar operator, relates the Pöschl-Teller parameter to the AdS/CFT dictionary via the nonlinear Fefferman-Graham map $z \propto z_{\mathrm{FG}}^{3}$. The $\mathcal{O}(\eps^{2})$ back-reaction yields explicit corrections to the renormalized free energy and Bekenstein-Hawking entropy density at fixed temperature, consistent with the first law; equivalently, at fixed entropy or energy density it \emph{lowers} the Hawking temperature by $δT/T=\mathcal{O}(\eps^{2})$. For the leading transport coefficients the shear viscosity saturates $η/s=1/(4π)$, while the Eling-Oz horizon formula gives a nonzero bulk viscosity $ζ/η=\bigl(\tfrac{Δ_{+}-3}{2}\bigr)^{2}\eps^{2}=\tfrac{3(7-\sqrt{33})}{8}\eps^{2}\approx0.471\,\eps^{2}$. With $c_{s}^{2}=\tfrac12-\mathcal O(\eps^{2})<\tfrac12$, this indicates a softening of the equation of state.

</details>


### [94] [Classical and Quantum Chaos from Foundations to Holography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19131)

TL;DR: 本文是由2026年钦奈数学研究所ST4研讨会六个讲座整理而成的讲义，系统阐述了从经典混沌定义到全息（holography）的混沌诊断学发展脉络，面向无混沌理论背景的读者，每个讲座配有习题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望从经典定义出发，系统地构建混沌诊断（diagnostics of chaos）的完整理论框架，将其从经典相空间轨迹的测量工具逐步推广到量子谱与全息背景下，使初学者能够无需先修知识即可掌握这一领域。

Method: 讲义分六个讲座层层递进：讲座1-2以几何方式定义经典混沌并构建基于相空间轨迹的诊断仪器；讲座3-4在量子化后转向从能谱和本征态重建工具（如能级统计、随机矩阵理论（random matrix theory）、谱形因子及本征态热化）；讲座5通过平方对易子恢复混沌速率，将混沌（scrambling）视为算符增长并追踪到穿越时间序对易子（out-of-time-order correlator）及混沌界；讲座6构造永恒黑洞、热场对偶态（thermofield double）与冲击波几何，并用两个探针区分黑洞视界无法辨别的背景。

Result: 该讲义揭示了混沌诊断在经典、量子与全息三个层面上的对应关系：经典层面测量的是相空间轨迹，量子层面以谱与本征态得到分类和计数而非速率，全息层面通过穿越时间序对易子恢复速率并触及蝴蝶速度（butterfly velocity）与混沌界（chaos bound）。此外，两个特定探针在黑洞背景区分中表现出超越视界结果的鉴别能力。

Conclusion: 本文为混沌诊断学提供了一个从经典到全息的完整、入门级教学路线图，指出不同层面的诊断工具各有其适用性与局限，并展示了在全息背景下新型探针相较于传统视界诊断的鉴别优势。

Abstract: These notes develop the diagnostics of chaos from their classical definitions through to holography, in six lectures given at the ST4 Workshop, Chennai Mathematical Institute, in 2026. No prior exposure to chaos theory is assumed. Lectures 1 and 2 define classical chaos geometrically and build the instruments that measure it, every one of which reads a phase-space trajectory. Quantization removes that trajectory, and Lectures 3 and 4 rebuild the instruments from spectra and eigenstates instead: level statistics, random matrix theory, the spectral form factor and eigenstate thermalization, as much the working toolkit for thermalization in many-body physics as for holography. What they return is a classification and a count rather than a rate. Lecture 5 recovers the rate from the squared commutator, treats scrambling as operator growth, follows the commutator to the out-of-time-order correlator, marks where that diagnostic stops being reliable, and reaches the butterfly velocity and the chaos bound. Lecture 6 constructs the eternal black hole, the thermofield double and the shock-wave geometry behind that bound, then follows two probes whose exponents discriminate between backgrounds where the horizon result cannot, one tracking a thermodynamic phase transition, the other resolving an anisotropy induced by a background field. Every lecture closes with exercises. Claude Opus 5, an AI assistant developed by Anthropic, worked as a research assistant under the author's direction.

</details>


### [95] [Maximal Enhancements in Eight-Dimensional Non-Supersymmetric Heterotic Strings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19166)
*Yamato Honda,Justin Kaidi,Yuichi Koga,Yuefeng Liu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过研究八维超对称heterotic弦在$T^2$上的orbifold，系统分类了八维中极大增强（maximally enhanced）、保秩（rank-preserving）的非超对称heterotic弦。从八维极大增强超对称Narain点出发，利用Kac定理枚举候选的阶二内作用，得到1210个极大增强点，其中71个无tachyon，25个既无tachyon也无平移扇区无质量标量。作者对每个点确定了规范代数及低能标量和费米子谱，对无tachyon情形计算了单圈宇宙学常数，对25个特殊子情形进一步计算了Hessian并检验了精化de Sitter沼泽地猜想（refined de Sitter swampland conjecture）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究的动机在于系统分类非超对称heterotic弦的极大增强点，此前九维情形已通过扩展Dynkin分析获得了95个极大半单增强点，作者希望将这一分类推广到八维，并考察真空能否满足弦理论中的沼泽地猜想约束。

Method: 作者首先回顾了九维构造过程作为验证基准，然后从八维极大增强超对称Narain点出发，利用Kac定理枚举候选的阶二内作用（order-two inner actions），仅保留能提升为一致Narain格位移（Narain-lattice shifts）的情形，从而构建非超对称orbifold模型。

Result: 分类得到1210个极大增强点，其中71个无tachyon，25个既无tachyon也无平移扇区无质量标量。对所有条目确定了规范代数及低能标量与费米子谱，对无tachyon情形计算了单圈宇宙学常数（one-loop cosmological constant），对25个特殊子情形计算了单圈势的Hessian并检验了精化de Sitter沼泽地猜想。

Conclusion: 该研究将非超对称heterotic弦的极大增强点分类从九维推广到八维，得到了大量新的真空结构，并通过宇宙学常数与Hessian计算对精化de Sitter沼泽地猜想进行了数值检验。

Abstract: We classify maximally enhanced, rank-preserving non-supersymmetric heterotic strings in eight dimensions by studying orbifolds of supersymmetric heterotic strings on $T^2$. We first review the construction in nine dimensions, where it reproduces the $95$ maximally semisimple enhancement points obtained from previous extended-Dynkin analyses. We then start from the maximally enhanced supersymmetric Narain points in eight dimensions and use Kac's theorem to enumerate candidate order-two inner actions, retaining only those that lift to consistent Narain-lattice shifts. This gives $1210$ maximal enhancement points, of which $71$ are tachyon-free, and $25$ have neither tachyons nor shifted-sector massless scalars. For each entry we determine the gauge algebra and the low-lying scalar and fermion spectra, and for the tachyon-free cases we evaluate the one-loop cosmological constant. For the 25 subcases without shifted-sector massless scalars, we further compute the Hessian of the one-loop potential and test the refined de Sitter swampland conjecture.

</details>

### [96] [The Cheeger constant of curved tubes in real space forms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18176)
*Petr Vlachopulos*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究了由闭合曲线定义的弯曲管状区域（curved tube）在任意维数的常截面曲率实空间形式（real space form）中的Cheeger常数（Cheeger constant），通过Fermi坐标系与测地球（geodesic sphere）的几何方法分别推导出上界与下界，并进一步计算了非紧实空间形式中无界弯曲管的Cheeger常数及其非有限体积Cheeger集的性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机源于弯曲管T(P,a)的几何性质，旨在求解该区域在任意维数常曲率空间中的Cheeger常数，这是一个在等周问题（isoperimetric problem）与谱几何中具有重要意义的几何不变量。

Method: 利用Fermi坐标系（Fermi coordinates）系统的结构性质对弯曲管进行参数化，借助面积与体积的精确公式直接计算Cheeger常数的上界；随后结合管的几何性质与校准型论证（calibration-type argument）推导下界，核心思想是用沿底层曲线移动的测地球来描述管，通过散度定理（divergence theorem）完成估计。

Result: 获得了任意维数常曲率实空间形式中弯曲管Cheeger常数的精确计算，包括上界与下界的完整推导；对非紧实空间形式中的无界弯曲管，同样计算了Cheeger常数，并证明了不存在有限体积的Cheeger集（finite-volume Cheeger set）。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了弯曲管在常曲率空间形式中Cheeger常数的完整解，并通过几何方法（Fermi坐标、测地球与散度定理）提供了一种结构性清晰且几何直观的计算框架，同时揭示了无界情形下Cheeger集的存在性限制。

Abstract: Motivated by the geometric properties of curved tubes $T\left(P,a\right)$ defined by closed curves $P$, we compute the Cheeger constant $h\left(T\left(P,a\right)\right)$ in the real space forms of arbitrary dimensions with constant sectional curvature. The structure and properties of the system of Fermi coordinates allows us to parametrize the curved tube and straightforwardly compute the upper bound of $h\left(T\left(P,a\right)\right)$ using the exact formulas for the area and volume of $T\left(P,a\right)$. Next, we derive the lower bound by combining the geometry of tubes with a calibration-type argument. The key idea is to describe the tube using geodesic spheres moving along the underlying curve $P$, which provides a natural outward direction and makes the estimate geometrically transparent. This allows us to compute the lower bound via the divergence theorem. Finally, for the class of unbounded curved tubes in noncompact real space forms, we also compute the Cheeger constant and prove that there is no finite-volume Cheeger set.

</details>


### [97] [Conformally Einstein anti-self-dual spaces and their generalisations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17847)
*Timothy Moy*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究了Grassmannian几何中爱因斯坦尺度（Einstein scales）与相容度量（compatible metrics）的存在性问题，重点包括反自对偶（ASD）共形结构下的共形到爱因斯坦问题，以及其推广到(2n,2)-Grassmannian结构的若干一般化情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在Grassmannian几何中，研究爱因斯坦尺度与相容度量的存在性是几何分析中的核心问题之一，其中ASD共形结构的共形到爱因斯坦问题在此前文献中已得到部分刻画。本文旨在在更一般的Weyl曲率泛性条件（genericity condition）下，给出更精细的局部存在性判定，并尝试将该问题推广至更高维的Grassmannian结构。

Method: 在ASD情形下，作者基于Weyl曲率的代数泛性条件推导局部爱因斯坦尺度存在的充要条件，并利用黎曼签名（Riemannian signature）下的ASD-Kähler度量结合全纯Killing向量场给出具体刻画。对于(2n,2)-Grassmannian结构（n>1），作者采用tractor演算（tractor calculus）构造相容度量存在性的代数障碍，并分析子最大对称模型（submaximally symmetric model）上线性独立相容度量的最大数目。

Result: 在ASD设定下获得了较文献中更精细的局部爱因斯坦尺度存在充要条件；证明了非Ricci-flat的ASD-Kähler度量在黎曼签名下局部共形爱因斯坦当且仅当其全纯Killing向量场的反自对偶导数与Ricci旋量成比例。对于(2n,2)-Grassmannian结构（n>1），在类似泛性条件下给出了局部爱因斯坦尺度存在的充要条件，并借助tractor演算获得相容度量存在的代数障碍，同时证明了该几何唯一的子最大对称模型恰好具有子最大数量的线性无关相容度量。

Conclusion: 本文系统扩展了Grassmannian几何中爱因斯坦尺度与相容度量存在性的研究，在ASD情形和高维Grassmannian情形下均给出了新的充要条件与代数障碍，并通过tractor演算揭示了对称模型与相容度量数目之间的精确关系。

Abstract: We study the existence of Einstein scales and compatible metrics in Grassmannian geometry. As a special case, this includes the conformal-to-Einstein problem for anti-self-dual (ASD) conformal structures.
  In the ASD setting, given a genericity condition algebraic in the Weyl curvature, we obtain necessary and sufficient conditions for the existence of a local Einstein scale that refine those previously appearing in the literature. We also prove that in Riemannian signature, a non-Ricci-flat ASD-Kähler metric is locally conformally Einstein if and only if it has a holomorphic Killing vector field with anti-self-dual derivative proportional to the Ricci spinor.
  Next, we consider some generalisations of the ASD conformal-to-Einstein problem for $(2n,2)$-Grassmannian structures. When $n > 1$, given an analogous genericity condition, we obtain necessary and sufficient conditions for the existence of a local Einstein scale. Using tractor calculus, we obtain algebraic obstructions to the existence of compatible metrics and show that the unique submaximally symmetric model of the geometry has the submaximal number of linearly independent compatible metrics.

</details>

### [98] [Engineering kekule superconductivity from layer-selective interactions in rhombohedral graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18337)
*Hung Dinh Nguyen,Yafis Barlas*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文通过平均场理论（mean-field theory）研究菱面体多层石墨烯（rhombohedral multilayer graphene）中的晶格尺度配对密度波（pair-density-wave）超导态，发现在保持时间反演和空间反演对称性的条件下，层/轨道依赖的配对相互作用与能带手性（band chirality）可诱导有限动量配对，形成具有相反手性的Kekulè超导凝聚体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在弱耦合下，有限动量超导通常被认为需要费米面破坏时间反演或空间反演对称性；本文旨在探究在两种对称性均保持的前提下，是否能通过层/轨道依赖的相互作用与狄拉克点费米面拓扑实现有限动量配对，从而拓宽有限动量超导态的实现途径。

Method: 采用平均场理论，比较有限动量扇区（$Q = \pm 2K_D$）与零动量（$Q=0$）超导态的能量；引入相反符号的层依赖相互作用（$V_{1A}=-V_{JB}=-|V|$）以及时间反演与空间反演对称性保持的Kane-Mele质量项（$\lambda$），并映射相图、分析临界温度$T_c$对手性指标$J$和$\lambda$的依赖；同时计算Kekulè超导态的超流刚度（superfluid stiffness）以确定BKT转变温度。

Result: 层依赖相互作用和能带手性在保持时间反演与空间反演对称性的前提下，诱导形成具有质心动量$Q=\pm 2 K_D$的谷内Kekulè态；该态仅在超过临界载流子密度$n^{crit}_{K}(\lambda,J)$时出现；两超导凝聚体对$K_D(-K_D)$谷具有相反手性$J(-J)$，从而保持两种对称性；研究还给出了相图、$T_c$对手性和Kane-Mele质量的依赖关系，以及BKT转变温度。

Conclusion: 轨道依赖的相互作用与能带手性相结合，可在保持时间反演和空间反演对称性的狄拉克材料中偏好有限动量配对，为在没有对称性破缺的情况下实现有限动量超导提供了新的物理机制。

Abstract: At weak coupling, finite-momentum superconductivity is typically associated with broken time-reversal or inversion symmetry of the Fermi surface. Here, we show that lattice-scale pair-density-wave order in rhombohedral multilayer graphene can arise from layer/orbital-dependent pairing interactions, band chirality, and Dirac-point-centered Fermi surface topology while preserving both symmetries. Using mean-field theory and comparing finite momentum sectors $Q = \pm 2 K_{D}$ with the $ Q = 0$ superconducting state, we find that layer-dependent interactions of opposite signs ($V_{1A}=-V_{JB}=-|V|$) favor an intra-valley Kekulè state with center-of-mass momentum ($ Q=\pm 2 K_D$). In the presence of a time-reversal and inversion symmetry-preserving Kane-Mele mass ($λ$), this state appears only above a critical carrier density ($n^{crit}_{K}(λ,J)$). The two superconducting condensates exhibit opposite chirality, $J(-J)$ for $K_D(-K_D) $ valleys, thereby preserving time-reversal and inversion symmetry. We map the phase diagram and analyze the dependence of $T_c$ on the chirality index $J$ and $λ$. We also evaluate the superfluid stiffness in the Kekulè superconducting state, thereby determining the Berezinskii-Kosterlitz-Thouless (BKT) transition temperature. Our results show that orbital-dependent interactions in the presence of band chirality favor finite-momentum pairing in time-reversal and inversion symmetric Dirac materials.

</details>


### [99] [Magnetism and Electrical Conduction in Lightly-Doped Single-Layer High-$T_c$ Cuprate $\mathrm{La}_2\mathrm{CuO}_{4+δ}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18563)
*Kota Miyakoshi,Yuya Kinugawa,Shusei Mizuta,Yoshihiko Ihara,Hiroyuki K. Yoshida,Tohru Kurosawa,Yasunori Toda,Naoki Momono,Migaku Oda*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文通过测量轻掺杂单层铜氧化物超导体La₂CuO₄₊δ（掺杂浓度p≈0.03）单晶的磁化强度、电阻率及其磁场依赖关系，并与极低掺杂区(p≲0.015)样品对比，考察了同时呈现反铁磁（antiferromagnetic, AF）和超导（superconducting, SC）态的Cu-O平面内禀磁性与导电行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示轻掺杂铜氧化物超导体中Cu-O平面同时呈现反铁磁与超导两种有序态时的本征磁性及电输运特性，弄清高掺杂下单层铜氧化物中高温反铁磁有序的可能机制。

Method: 对La₂CuO₄₊δ单晶进行磁化强度、电阻率及其磁场依赖关系的温度依赖测量，将p≈0.03超导样品与p≲0.015极低掺杂区样品进行对比，分析Cu位次格子磁矩、反铁磁耦合强度以及二维反铁磁自旋关联随温度的变化。

Result: 在p≈0.03超导样品中，Cu位次格子磁矩及反铁磁耦合仅比Mott绝缘母体小约15%，表明Cu 3d电子局域化仍很强；二维反铁磁自旋关联从T*≈280 K迅速发展到Néel温度T_N=266 K，面外电阻率在此时开始显著下降。

Conclusion: 超导单层铜氧化物在p≈0.03下仍能维持如此高的反铁磁有序温度，可能与T*至T_N区间内二维反铁磁自旋关联的快速建立以及伴随的面外电阻率骤降有关。

Abstract: The temperature dependences of magnetization and electrical resistivity as well as their magnetic field dependences have been examined in lightly-doped single-layer cuprate $\mathrm{La}_2\mathrm{CuO}_{4+δ}$ (LCO, hole-doping level $p \, (2δ) \cong 0.03$) single crystals, in comparison with those in the extremely low doping region of $p \lesssim 0.015$ to uncover the intrinsic magnetism and electrical conduction of the $\mathrm{Cu\text{-}O}$ plane that exhibits both antiferromagnetic (AF) and superconducting (SC) orders simultaneously. In $p \cong 0.03$ SC LCO, the sub-lattice moments on $\mathrm{Cu}$ sites and their AF couplings are only $\sim 15\,\%$ smaller than those of the Mott-insulator parent material, suggesting that the localization of $\mathrm{Cu}$ $3d$ electrons remains very strong. Furthermore, we report that in the SC LCO, two-dimensional AF spin correlations develop rapidly from $T^* \cong 280\text{ K}$ towards Néel temperature $T_{\mathrm{N}} = 266\text{ K}$, where the out-of-plane resistivity starts to decrease largely. This might be responsible for the AF ordering at such a high temperature in the SC single-layer cuprate with $p \cong 0.03$.

</details>


### [100] [Predicting critical temperature in quantum simulators for high-$T_c$ superconductivity: the matrix product state plus mean field approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18861)
*Thomas Köhler,Adrian Kantian*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文以超冷原子晶格气体平台为基础，探讨近期提出并部分实现的混合维度（mixD）模型在实现高温超导（high-Tc superconductivity）方面的潜力。作者利用矩阵乘积态加平均场理论（MPS+MF）研究了具有各向异性隧穿的二维mixD模型，并系统表征了构成其子单元的一维mixD系统，为构建二维高温超导相奠定了基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于超冷原子晶格气体的量子模拟是探索高温超导的重要平台，但自该领域起步以来，在传统计算机上实现高温超导态仍远超量子多体数值计算的能力，也尚未在实验中真正实现。为此，本文旨在利用近期提出的混合维度（mixD）模型——该模型已证明可实现极高配对能且保持可观的对迁移率——来推进该目标的实现。

Method: 作者针对费米子系统，采用矩阵乘积态加平均场理论（MPS+MF）方法，研究具有各向异性隧穿的二维mixD模型的高温超导潜力。同时，对构成二维系统子单元的一维mixD系统进行全面表征，覆盖当前实验相关的系统尺寸范围，并建立实用启发式判据来确定有限尺寸效应何时会妨碍利用一维mixD系统构建二维系统。

Result: 研究表明，二维mixD模型有可能进入高温超导相。此外，通过对一维mixD系统的综合表征，作者确定了在实际可达的实验系统尺寸下，有限尺寸效应制约二维系统构建的具体条件，即相关启发式判据。

Conclusion: 该工作为利用mixD模型在超冷原子平台上实现高温超导态奠定了理论基础，展示了二维各向异性隧穿mixD模型作为通往高温超导相的可行途径，并为一维子单元的适用边界提供了实用判据。

Abstract: Quantum simulation based on ultra cold atomic lattice gases is one of the most promising platforms to investigate high-$T_c$ superconductivity beyond the limited capabilities of quantum many body numerics on classical computers. Yet, despite enormous progress since the field's inception, realizing a high-$T_c$ superconducting state still remains out of reach. The present work lays the groundwork to purpose the recently proposed, and already partly realized, mixed-dimensional (mixD) models, towards this end. These systems offer the proven capability to realize very high pairing energies while retaining appreciable mobility of pairs. We specifically investigate the potential of 2D mixD-models with anisotropic tunneling, using the matrix product state plus mean field theory (MPS+MF) for fermions, and show that these models may enter a high-$T_c$ superconducting phase. These simulations in turn are based on a comprehensive characterization of the 1D mixD-systems, which are the sub-units of which the 2D system is comprized. In this, we cover the range of currently experimentally relevant system sizes, and establish practical heuristics to determine when finite\hyp size effects preclude the use of a 1D mixD-system to build the 2D ones.

</details>


### [101] [Synthesis and stability of high-$T_c$ LaH$_{10\pmδ}$ films at high pressures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18865)
*Sam Cross,William Thomas,Lawrence Nobbs,Rebecca Nicholls,Oliver Lord,Qian Zhang,Dominique Laniel,Max Gerin,Bjorn Wehinger,Mohamed Mezouar,Xiaojiao Liu,Egor Koemets,Annette Kleppe,Sven Friedemann,Jonathan Buhot*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本研究通过物理气相沉积（PVD）制备镧（La）薄膜前驱体，以氨硼烷（NH₃BH₃）为氢源，在金刚石压砧（DAC）中于168 GPa和176 GPa压力下原位激光加热合成了LaH₁₀±δ薄膜，并通过同步辐射X射线衍射与电学测量证实其高临界温度（T_c ≈ 247 K）超导电性，且样品在约300天内保持结构和高Tc超导电性的稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高压氢化物是目前所有超导体类别中拥有最高超导临界温度的体系，其中LaH₁₀在140-180 GPa压力下达到约250 K的最高二元临界温度（T_c）。然而此前研究主要基于块体样品，缺乏一种可控、可扩展的合成途径，限制了向三元氢化物和器件集成方向的发展。本研究旨在通过薄膜前驱体路线，为氢化物合成提供实用且可调控的新方法。

Method: 采用物理气相沉积（physical vapour deposition, PVD）技术制备元素镧（La）薄膜前驱体，并将其与氨硼烷（NH₃BH₃）作为氢供体装载于两个金刚石压砧（diamond anvil cell, DAC）中，在168 GPa和176 GPa压力下通过原位（in situ）激光加热实现LaH₁₀±δ薄膜的合成。利用同步辐射X射线衍射解析高对称性面心立方（fcc）镧亚晶格（空间群Fm̄3m），并通过电学测量及磁场下超导抑制行为确认高Tc超导电性。

Result: 在176 GPa下测得最高临界温度T_c ≈ 247 K，与块体LaH₁₀结果一致；衍射数据确认fcc镧亚晶格及其晶胞参数与前期块体样品研究高度吻合。磁场中观察到超导电性的特征性抑制现象。联合衍射与电学测量显示，激光加热后约300天的整个测量周期内，晶体结构与高Tc超导电性均保持显著的时间稳定性。

Conclusion: 本研究确立了基于PVD技术的薄膜前驱体作为氢化物形成实用路线的可行性，为可控合成有前景的三元氢化物以及在金刚石压砧中集成微加工器件几何构型开辟了新途径。

Abstract: High-pressure hydrides hold the record for the highest superconducting critical temperatures across all classes of superconductors. Currently lanthanum decahydride, LaH$_{10}$, exhibits the highest critical temperature among binaries, with $T_c \approx$ 250 K at pressures between 140-180 GPa. Here, we report the synthesis of LaH$_{10\pmδ}$ films in two DACs at pressures of 168 GPa and 176 GPa via in situ laser heating of elemental lanthanum films with ammonia borane (NH$_3$BH$_3$) as the hydrogen donor. The high-symmetry fcc lanthanum sublattice (space group $Fm\bar3m$) is resolved using synchrotron X-ray diffraction, with unit cell parameters in excellent agreement with previous studies on bulk samples. We provide confirmation of high-$T_c$ superconductivity in LaH$_{10\pmδ}$ with highest $T_c$ of 247 K at 176 GPa evidenced in electrical measurements. The characteristic suppression of superconductivity is observed in magnetic fields. Furthermore, combined diffraction and electrical measurements reveal remarkable temporal stability of both the crystal structure and the high-$T_c$ superconductivity over the full measurement period of about 300 days post laser heating. Our work establishes film precursors using physical vapour deposition (PVD) techniques as a practical route to hydride formation, opening a pathway toward the controlled synthesis of promising ternary hydrides and the integration of micro-fabricated device geometries in diamond anvil cells.

</details>


### [102] [Emergent BEC mechanism in flat-band superconductors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19044)
*V. Berger,I. S. Tupitsyn,B. Currie,B. V. Svistunov,E. Kozik,N. V. Prokof'ev*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该研究通过受控精度的数值方法，证实了在平带与下带接触的吸引性Lieb晶格模型中，玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚（BEC）机制仍然稳健且高效：相互作用诱导能隙保护了费米子束缚对，且束缚对具有异常轻的有效质量，从而产生极高的超导临界温度T_c。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上，平带超导性的BEC机制依赖于平带与占据下带之间存在能隙；但当非相互作用的平带与下带接触时（如吸引性Lieb晶格模型），该图像失效，束缚态的存续与位置变得不确定。作者旨在澄清在此类带接触情形下束缚对的命运及其对超导温度的影响。

Method: 采用受控精度的数值协议（controlled-precision numerical protocol），作者证明了该方法在此类本质上非微扰（non-perturbative）的问题中是必需的，并基于此研究掺杂进入平带的费米子在相互作用下形成束缚对的行为。

Result: 结果表明，尽管平带与下带接触，BEC机制仍然普遍稳健：费米子在平带内掺杂后，在相互作用诱导的能隙内形成束缚对，且这些束缚对具有异常轻的有效质量（anomalously light effective mass），从而支持极高的T_c。

Conclusion: 在平带与下带接触的系统中，相互作用诱导的能隙足以保护束缚对并支撑BEC机制，且由于束缚对有效质量极轻，可实现异常高的超导临界温度，推翻了传统上认为此类系统不适合BEC图像的观点。

Abstract: The formation of bound bosonic pairs of fermions, followed by their Bose-Einstein (quasi) condensation (BEC), is a foundational mechanism of superconductivity. At low filling, flat-band superconductivity is well captured by this mechanism provided the flat band is separated from the occupied lower band by an energy gap. However, particularly high $T_c$ values are anticipated when the non-interacting flat and lower bands touch---as in the prototypical attractive Lieb-lattice model studied here---invalidating the conventional picture: while interactions might protect the bound state by opening a gap, no small parameter guarantees the separation of the bound-state energy from this gap or occupied-band excitations, leaving the pair's fate uncertain. Based on a controlled-precision numerical protocol---which we demonstrate to be essential in this fundamentally non-perturbative problem---we show that the BEC mechanism, underpinned by an interaction-induced gap, is generically robust and remarkably efficient: fermions doped into the flat band form bound pairs within this gap with an anomalously light effective mass, enabling an exceptionally high $T_c$.

</details>


### [103] [Ultrasonic attenuation in inhomogeneous superconductors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19079)
*Aman Sardwal,Andreas Kreisel,Shantanu Mukherjee*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文通过实空间Bogoliubov–de Gennes方法研究超导体系中亚隙束缚态（subgap bound states）对超声衰减（ultrasonic attenuation）的贡献。研究发现相干因子（coherence factors）充当滤波器，使不同对称性和自旋结构的束缚态在超声衰减中产生截然不同的信号：相位偏置SNS结中的Andreev束缚态呈现共振峰，而d波超导体(110)晶界处的杂质诱导束缚态因相干因子近乎抵消而信号微弱，仅在局域磁性形成时通过自旋劈裂产生显著衰减峰。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 亚隙束缚态可在态密度（density of states）中产生显著特征，却不必然在超声衰减中对应出现信号。这一差异提示束缚态的内部结构与相干因子对其物理可观测性具有重要影响，促使本文系统研究不同对称性和自旋结构的束缚态在超声衰减中的表现及其区分机制。

Method: 作者采用实空间Bogoliubov–de Gennes（BdG）公式化方法描述超声衰减，并在均匀s波和d波超导体中验证了该方法在低温下的正确性。通过分析不同体系中束缚态的相干因子及其对声子衰变矩阵元的影响，分别计算了相位偏置SNS结和(110)晶界两类型体系中的超声衰减响应。

Result: 在相位偏置超导体–正常金属–超导体（SNS）结中，当声子频率与相位依赖的亚隙能级间距相匹配时，Andreev束缚态产生超声衰减共振。相比之下，d_{x²−y²}超导体(110)晶界处非磁杂质诱导的束缚态尽管具有巨大的谱权重，其相干因子近乎抵消，导致衰减信号微弱；而一旦局域磁性在晶界附近形成，相干抵消消失、束缚态发生自旋劈裂，则出现清晰的衰减峰，该过程在低温下由准粒子对发射声子所主导。

Conclusion: 超声衰减能够区分谱特征相似但对称性和自旋结构不同的束缚态——相干因子在其中扮演关键的滤波角色。这一发现表明超声探测可以作为态密度以外、更具结构分辨力的束缚态表征手段。

Abstract: Subgap bound states can produce pronounced features in the density of states without necessarily giving rise to a corresponding signal in ultrasonic attenuation. We show that the coherence factors act as a filter for the contribution of bound states to ultrasonic attenuation. In a phase-biased superconductor--normal--superconductor junction, the Andreev bound states produce a resonance in the attenuation when the phonon frequency matches the phase-dependent separation between the subgap levels. The situation is different at a $(110)$ grain boundary in a $d_{x^2-y^2}$ superconductor. There, nonmagnetic impurity-induced bound states remain weak in the attenuation despite their large spectral weight, because the relevant coherence factors nearly cancel. When local magnetism develops near the boundary, this cancellation is removed, the bound states become spin split, and a clear attenuation peak appears, which we argue is governed by a process involving emission of phonons by pair of quasiparticles at low temperatures. We obtain these results using a real-space Bogoliubov--de Gennes formulation of ultrasonic attenuation and benchmark the method against the known low-temperature behavior of homogeneous $s$- and $d$-wave superconductors. The results show that ultrasound can distinguish bound states with similar spectral signatures but different symmetry and spin structure.

</details>


### [104] [Large Field-Free Superconducting Diode Effect with Nonmonotonic Polarity Reversals in NbSe$_2$/CrBr$_3$ Heterostructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19099)
*Shengbiao Sun,Lang Xiao,Chenghao Shen,Bowen Hao,Jia-Peng Peng,Ziye Zhu,Feiyue Wang,Qilin Han,Ya-Qing Bie,Shuo Wang,Tong Zhou,Dapeng Yu,Ben-Chuan Lin*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本研究在范德瓦尔斯异质结NbSe₂/CrBr₃中实现了效率高达35.7%的零场超导二极管效应（field-free superconducting diode effect），其极性可由磁场历史编程，并揭示该效应源于库珀对动量奇宇称的立方对称项以及CrBr₃层状亚铁磁性产生的界面交换场。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超导二极管效应（superconducting diode effect）有望实现超低功耗超导电子器件，但多数实现依赖外加磁场，在零场下获得大且可控的SDE仍具挑战。作者旨在探索一种无需外加磁场即可实现高效、可编程SDE的机制和材料平台。

Method: 作者在NbSe₂/CrBr₃范德瓦尔斯异质结中进行输运测量，并辅以微磁模拟（micromagnetic simulation）和推广的Ginzburg-Landau理论，分析了零场下SDE的极性反转行为及其与CrBr₃层状磁结构的关联。

Result: 观察到零场SDE效率达35.7%，极性可通过磁场历史编程；垂直磁场扫描下二极管极性呈现非单调且滞后的反转。该效应无法用Meissner屏蔽或常规铁磁邻近效应解释，其根源为CrBr₃中层状亚铁磁性（layered ferrimagnetism）产生的历史依赖界面交换场。

Conclusion: 该工作提出了非互易超导电性的新机制，即由库珀对动量奇宇称的对称允许三次项驱动的不对等临界电流，同时平衡凝聚体保持零动量；并确立层状范德瓦尔斯磁性材料作为高效、可编程、零场超导二极管的多功能平台。

Abstract: The superconducting diode effect (SDE), characterized by nonreciprocal dissipationless supercurrent, offers a promising route toward ultralow-power superconducting electronics. Yet most realizations require an external magnetic field, and achieving a large, controllable SDE at zero field remains challenging. Here we report a field-free SDE with an efficiency reaching 35.7% in a van der Waals NbSe$_2$/CrBr$_3$ heterostructure, whose polarity is programmable by magnetic-field history. Unlike conventional mechanisms based on finite-momentum pairing, the SDE originates from the leading symmetry-allowed cubic term odd in Cooper-pair momentum, which yields unequal critical currents while leaving the equilibrium condensate at zero momentum. Remarkably, upon sweeping a perpendicular magnetic field, the diode polarity undergoes nonmonotonic and hysteretic reversals that cannot be explained by Meissner screening or conventional ferromagnetic proximity. Combining transport measurements, micromagnetic simulations, and a generalized Ginzbur-Landau theory, we attribute these unconventional behaviors to layered ferrimagnetism in CrBr$_3$, where coexisting ferromagnetic and antiferromagnetic interlayer couplings produce a history-dependent interfacial exchange field acting on NbSe$_2$. Our results reveal a distinct mechanism for nonreciprocal superconductivity and establish layered van der Waals magnetism as a versatile platform for high-efficiency, programmable, field-free superconducting diodes.

</details>

### [105] [Search for anomalous couplings in WW and WZ production with single-lepton final states in proton-proton collisions at $\sqrt{s}$ = 13 TeV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18245)
*CMS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 利用CMS探测器记录的质子-质子对撞数据（质心能量13 TeV，积分亮度138 fb⁻¹），通过有效场论（effective field theory）方法搜寻标准模型（standard model）之外的异常耦合信号，对与反常规范玻色子自耦合及矢量玻色子-夸克耦合对应的维数为六的有效场论算符的Wilson系数施加了目前最严格的约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准模型虽在实验上高度成功，但仍可能存在由新物理引起的偏离。通过在双玻色子（diboson）产生过程中直接约束反常耦合的Wilson系数，可以检验标准模型的完整性并寻找超出标准模型的新物理信号。

Method: 采用基于有效场论方法的唯象学分析，利用CMS探测器在LHC上记录的13 TeV质子-质子对撞数据（积分亮度138 fb⁻¹）；研究WW和WZ双玻色子产生过程，其中一个W玻色子轻子衰变（衰变为轻子加反中微子或电荷共轭态），另一个W或Z玻色子强子衰变；针对高能区域中强子衰变产物合并为单个大半径喷注（large-radius jet）的末态，采用基于机器学习的专用分类器将强子化W和Z玻色子衰变从背景过程中分离。

Result: 报道了对对应于反常三重规范玻色子耦合（anomalous triple gauge boson couplings）的算符Wilson系数迄今最严格的约束；对矢量玻色子-夸克耦合所设的界限与此前的全包容喷注测量结果具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 该研究通过有效场论框架有效利用高能量区域的双玻色子产生数据，显著提升了对比标准模型偏离的探测敏感度，为限制超出标准模型的新物理提供了重要实验约束。

Abstract: A search for deviations from the standard model using an effective field theory approach is carried out using the proton-proton collision data set recorded by the CMS experiment at the LHC at a center-of-mass energy of 13 TeV, corresponding to an integrated luminosity of 138 fb$^{-1}$. In this study Wilson coefficients corresponding to dimension-six effective field theory operators that would lead to anomalous gauge boson self-couplings and modified couplings between vector bosons and quarks are constrained. Diboson (WW and WZ) production processes with one W boson decaying to a lepton plus antineutrino or charge conjugate and the second W or Z boson decaying hadronically are considered. Since the contribution from anomalous couplings is expected to be most visible at high energy scales, the focus is on final states where the hadronic decay products of a W or Z boson are merged into a single large-radius jet. A dedicated classifier based on machine learning is employed to separate hadronic W and Z boson decays from background processes. The most stringent constraints to date on the Wilson coefficients of operators corresponding to anomalous triple gauge boson couplings are reported. The bounds set on the couplings between vector bosons and quarks are competitive with those from previous inclusive jet measurements.

</details>


### [106] [Performance of heavy-flavour jet identification in the CMS high-level trigger in proton-proton collisions at $\sqrt{s}$ = 13.6 TeV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18331)
*CMS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文介绍了CMS实验在2022至2024年间，于高级触发器（HLT）中部署和优化的、基于深度学习的重夸克喷注标识算法，其设计、调试与性能显著提升了多类关键物理过程的信号选择效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CMS触发系统需要将LHC产生的高碰撞率降低至可存储和离线分析的kHz级别，同时保持对包含重夸克（b和c）喷注过程的高选择效率。这要求开发专门的喷注味标识方法并在高级触发器（HLT）中实现优化，以满足物理分析需求。

Method: 在质子-质子对撞能量为√s=13.6 TeV的条件下，针对CMS高级触发器（HLT）设计了基于深度学习的喷注标识算法，并完成系统的设计、调试（commissioning）与性能评估。

Result: 新算法在多个关键物理过程中实现了信号效率的显著提升，包括非共振希格斯玻色子对衰变至四个b夸克的过程，以及经由矢量玻色子融合和伴随tt对产生的希格斯玻色子在H→bb和H→cc衰变道中的产生过程。

Conclusion: 基于深度学习的喷注标识算法在HLT中的部署证明了其能够在保持可持续触发输出率的同时，有效提升重夸克相关物理过程的信号选择能力，为2022-2024年间CMS实验的关键物理分析提供了有力支撑。

Abstract: The CMS trigger system plays a crucial role during data taking, reducing the large collision rate delivered by the LHC to a few kHz for data storage and subsequent offline analysis. The system aims to maintain a high selection efficiency for a wide range of processes, including those involving jets originating from heavy-flavour quarks (b and c), which provide a distinctive signature in many physics analyses. To achieve this while maintaining a sustainable trigger output rate, dedicated jet flavour identification methods are developed and optimized for use in the high-level trigger (HLT). This paper presents the design, commissioning, and performance of deep-learning-based jet identification algorithms deployed in the HLT during 2022$-$2024, for proton-proton collisions at $\sqrt{s}$ = 13.6 TeV. The new algorithms enabled significant improvements in signal efficiency for a variety of key physics processes, including the non-resonant production of Higgs boson pairs decaying to four b quarks, as well as Higgs boson production via both vector boson fusion and in association with a $\mathrm{t\bar{t}}$ pair, in the H $\to$ $\mathrm{b\bar{b}}$ and H $\to$ $\mathrm{c\bar{c}}$ decay channels.

</details>


### [107] [Exact time-correlated coincidence modeling with reset boundaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18728)
*Jinjing Li*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文为延迟符合（delayed-coincidence）搜索中记录的提示-延迟（prompt-delayed）事件序列推导了精确的有序符合率，考虑了μ子否决（muon veto）、事件死时间（dead time）和泊松重置边界（Poisson reset boundaries）等复杂条件，并通过矩阵解析工具（matrix-analytic toolkit）实现了窗内积分的闭式求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 延迟符合搜索中的偶然本底（accidental background）并非边缘率的简单乘积，因为μ子否决、事件死时间和窗条件会以复杂方式限制事件序列的记录方式，需要精确的符合率计算方法。

Method: 在给定随机模型下，将计算分离为两部分：马尔可夫历史链（Markov history chain）在重置边界处携带仍待处理的延迟事件跨窗传递；当前窗口传播子（current-window propagator）利用块矩阵指数（block-matrix exponentials）以闭式形式求有序窗内积分。该构造可通过增加块链深度扩展到任意有限重数（finite multiplicity）。

Result: 报告了一至三重的所有有序率（由非相关单例、相关提示事件和记录的延迟事件构成），以及提示-延迟对的真实/偶然分割、多重性效率（multiplicity efficiencies）和四重及以上的事件聚合率；对默认窗关闭约定导出了三项后验截断误差界（a posteriori error bound），并通过流式玩具蒙特卡洛（toy Monte Carlo）验证了结果。

Conclusion: 在所陈述的假设范围内，该构造在保留的有限状态空间上精确成立，并提供了显式可计算的截断误差界。

Abstract: Delayed-coincidence searches identify a rare prompt-delayed signal, but their accidental background is not a simple product of marginal rates: muon vetoes, event dead time, and delayed events created before the current window condition which event sequences can be recorded. We derive exact ordered coincidence rates, within a stated stochastic model, for a recorded stream of uncorrelated prompt-like singles and correlated prompt-delayed sources subject to Poisson reset boundaries and event dead time. The calculation separates two tasks: a Markov history chain carries the delayed events still pending at reset boundaries across windows, and a current-window propagator evaluates the ordered within-window integrals in closed form with block-matrix exponentials, the matrix-analytic toolkit of applied probability. The construction extends to any prescribed finite multiplicity by increasing the block-chain depth. Here we report every ordered one-, two-, and three-fold rate formed from uncorrelated singles, correlated prompts, and recorded delayed events, together with the genuine/accidental split of prompt-delayed pairs, multiplicity efficiencies, and the aggregate rate for multiplicity four or more. For the default window-close convention, we derive a three-term a posteriori error bound for the finite pending-population truncation. An independent streaming toy Monte Carlo validates the ordered rates, two-fold time densities, matched dead-time conventions, and aggregate high multiplicity. Within the stated assumptions, the construction is exact on the retained finite state spaces and provides explicit, computable truncation-error bounds.

</details>


### [108] [Technical Proposal for the Atom Interferometer CERN Experiment (AICE) Facility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18743)
*Gianluigi Arduini,Nadja Augst,Mark G. Bason,Charles Baynham,Andrea Bertoldi,Gianfranco Bertone,Diego Blas,Daniela Bortoletto,Sougato Bose,Roberto Ales Bozzi,Oliver Buchmueller,Tamara Alice Bud,Clare Burrage,Sergio Calatroni,John Carlton,Vassilis Charmandaris,Maria Luisa Chiofalo,Pierre Cladé,Jonathon Coleman,Fabio Corsanego,Albert De Roeck,Arnaud Devienne,Fabio Di Pumpo,John Ellis,Pierre Fayet,Chris Foot,Elina Fuchs,Naceur Gaaloul,Susan Gardner,Enno Giese,Eduardo Granados,Saïda Guellati-Khelifa,Michael Guinchard,Timo Hakulinen,Tiffany Harte,Richard Hobson,Michael Holynski,Angelo Infantino,Alex Kehagias,Eva Kilian-Rademacher,Wolfgang Korsch,Damien Lafarge,Samuel Lellouch,Lucas Lombriser,Elias Lopez Asamar,J. Luis Lopez-Gonzalez,Michele Maggiore,Charline Marcel,Anna Marchant,Christopher McCabe,Gaetano Mileti,Peter Millington,Jeremiah Mitchell,Noam Mouelle,Angel Navascues Cornago,François Pillon,Rosa Poggiani,Johann Rafelski,Ernst M. Rasel,Maike Reckermann,Albert Roura,Rui Samoes,Federico Sanchez Nieto,Jack Sander,Carlo Scarcia,Jesse Schelfhout,Wolfgang Schleich,Dennis Schlippert,Ulrich Schneider,Steven Schramm,Florian Schreck,Olga Sergijenko,Marcelle Soares-Santos,Guglielmo M. Tino,Jonathan N. Tinsley,Tristan Valenzuela,Daniel Valuch,Maurits van der Grinten,Klaasjan van Druten,Ville Vaskonen,Wolf von Klitzing,Michael Werner*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文提出了CERN原子干涉仪实验（AICE）的技术方案，这是一个安装在LHC PX46竖井内约100米高的垂直原子干涉仪。AICE的主要科学目标是探测其他实验无法触及的质量范围内玻色子超轻暗物质（ULDM），次要目标是作为未来更长基线探测器的探路者，探索约0.03–3 Hz频率范围的引力波（GWs）。实验采用^87Sr原子的单光子698 nm干涉仪，并计划发展^88Sr和^171Yb升级路线，可行性研究确认PX46场地技术成熟、无阻碍，预计LS3期间完成场地准备后可安装运行且不影响HL-LHC运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超轻暗物质（ULDM）在我国现有仪器可探测的质量范围之外仍存在未探索区域，需要更长的基线原子干涉仪来覆盖这一空白。同时，重力波（GWs）在中频段（约0.03–3 Hz）的探测尚无成熟的实验方案，需要探路者装置积累技术经验。

Method: AICE采用垂直约100米基线的多源梯度仪（multi-source gradiometer）几何构型，初始配置使用三个竖井原子源和698 nm单光子跃迁的极冷^87Sr原子干涉仪，另设一个地表参考源用于激光稳定与诊断。后续计划切换至^88Sr以探测轴子类粒子（ALPs）、矢量ULDM及等效原理违背，并通过^171Yb升级进一步提高敏感度。

Result: 概念可行性研究与详细技术实施方案研究表明，PX46场地成熟度高且具备实施条件，无技术瓶颈。在LS3期间完成场地准备后即可安装运行AICE，且不会影响HL-LHC的正常运行。该探测器设计建立在VLBAI、MAGIS实验和AION-10技术设计报告基础上，将锶梯度仪架构扩展到约100米基线，并获得由22个国家57个机构组成的TVLBAI原协作组支持。

Conclusion: AICE作为一个多功能长基线原子干涉设施，能够在ULDM探测和引力波路径探索两个方向上发挥作用，其场地准备和安装进程与CERN的长期运行计划兼容，是当前技术成熟度最高的候选方案。

Abstract: We present the technical proposal for the Atom Interferometer CERN Experiment (AICE), a $\mathcal{O}(100)$ m vertical atom interferometer to be installed against the wall of the PX46 access shaft to the LHC. AICE is conceived as a versatile and flexible long-baseline atom-interferometry facility whose primary scientific goal is probing for bosonic ultralight dark matter (ULDM) in a mass range inaccessible to other experiments, with a secondary goal of pioneering the exploration of gravitational waves (GWs) with frequencies in the range ${\sim}$0.03-3 Hz as a pathfinder for future longer-baseline detectors. The initial configuration employs ultracold $^{87}$Sr atoms in a single-photon 698-nm interferometer with three shaft-based atom sources in a multi-source gradiometer geometry, supported by one surface reference source for laser stabilisation and diagnostics, to target scalar ULDM. Operation with $^{88}$Sr will give sensitivity to axion-like particles (ALPs), vector ULDM with $B-L$ couplings and violation of the principle of equivalence, while a $^{171}$Yb upgrade will improve the sensitivity to $B-L$ couplings and equivalence violations. Probing the Einstein equivalence principle (EP) and measuring $α$ will proceed in parallel with the ULDM searches. A conceptual feasibility study and a detailed technical implementation study have established that PX46 is a uniquely mature and implementation-ready site, with no technical showstoppers. Completing site preparation works during LS3 would enable the subsequent installation and operation of AICE without impacting HL-LHC operations. The detector design builds on the VLBAI and MAGIS experiments and the AION-10 Technical Design Report, scaling the strontium gradiometer architecture to the $\sim$100 m baseline. AICE is endorsed by the TVLBAI Proto-Collaboration, comprising 57 institutions in 22 countries.

</details>


### [109] [FAST-HEP: Compiling Declarative Analysis Workflows for High-Energy Physics and Beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18745)
*Luke Kreczko*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文提出FAST-HEP及其工作流引擎Flow，通过将声明式工作流语言、编译器与运行时结合，分离工作流的科学描述与实现/执行，利用编译技术实现高能物理计算工作流的可移植性、可扩展性与可复现性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高能物理（high-energy physics）分析依赖复杂软件工作流，其科学寿命往往超过底层软件生态系统的寿命；在软件、数据格式和执行环境不断演化的同时维持可复现性（reproducibility）是一项重大挑战，且该挑战并非高能物理领域独有，数据密集型科学分析普遍面临。

Method: FAST-HEP的Flow引擎结合声明式工作流语言、编译器和运行时，将工作流的科学描述与其实现及执行分离；通过规范化、图构建、依赖分析和执行规划将工作流编译为与后端无关的执行计划，再由统一运行时利用可替换能力编排执行。该架构支持静态验证、分析操作/执行后端/存储技术的模块化替换，并在编译和执行全程记录溯源（provenance）。

Result: Flow的工作流模型和编排层虽为高能物理分析需求开发，但具有领域无关（domain-independent）特性；通过将编译技术应用于科学分析工作流，实现了透明、可扩展、可移植且可复现的工作流基础，使科学分析与其支撑软件生态系统能够独立演化和相互解耦。

Conclusion: FAST-HEP为科学分析工作流提供了基于编译器技术的通用架构基础，使高能物理及其他数据密集型领域的分析软件与其底层软件生态系统可以独立演变，以应对软件生命周期不匹配带来的可复现性挑战。

Abstract: High-energy physics analyses increasingly rely on complex software workflows whose scientific lifetime often exceeds that of the underlying software ecosystem. Maintaining reproducibility while accommodating evolving analysis software, data formats, and execution environments therefore remains a significant challenge. These challenges are not unique to high-energy physics and are shared by many data-intensive scientific analyses.
  We present FAST-HEP and its workflow engine, Flow, which combines a declarative workflow language, compiler, and runtime. Flow separates the scientific description of a workflow from its implementation and execution, and compiles workflows into backend-independent execution plans through normalization, graph construction, dependency analysis, and execution planning. A common runtime then orchestrates the resulting plan using replaceable capabilities. This architecture enables static validation and modular replacement of analysis operations, execution backends, and storage technologies, while recording provenance throughout compilation and execution.
  Although developed for the requirements of high-energy physics analysis, Flow's workflow model and orchestration layer are domain-independent. By applying compiler techniques to scientific analysis workflows, FAST-HEP provides a foundation for workflows that are transparent, extensible, portable, and reproducible, allowing scientific analyses and their supporting software ecosystems to evolve independently.

</details>


### [110] [Exploring the limits of high-energy proton-pion separation in granular calorimeters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19064)
*Andrea De Vita,Abhishek,Tommaso Dorigo,Pietro Vischia*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本研究使用Geant4模拟，在均匀钨酸铅量能器中利用深度集（Deep Sets）模型，基于细胞位置、能量和时间信息区分质子与正π介子，10 GeV时准确率达93.8%，100 GeV时降至67.2%；同时考察了探测器分段细度和粒子能量对鉴别能力的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高粒度量能器（highly granular calorimeters）能提供强子簇射（hadronic-shower）演化的详细信息，可能超越其在能量测量中的传统角色，实现粒子鉴别（particle identification）。作者希望探究这种信息在区分质子和正π介子方面的能力，以及探测器分段细度（segmentation）和粒子能量如何影响鉴别效果。

Method: 利用Geant4模拟能量为10至100 GeV的孤立粒子在均匀钨酸铅（lead-tungstate）量能器中产生的响应。构建一个深度集（Deep Sets）模型，直接作用于细胞位置、探测能量和时间信息，并与基于重建簇射观测量（shower observables）的增强决策树（boosted decision tree）进行比较。

Result: 深度集模型在10 GeV下达到93.8%的准确率，在100 GeV时降至67.2%。簇射拓扑（shower topology）独立提供区分信息，沉积能量（deposited energy）提供最大的附加贡献，时间信息提供互补信息。较粗的分段（coarser segmentation）会降低鉴别能力，其中性能对纵向粒度（longitudinal granularity）比对横向粒度（transverse granularity）更为敏感。

Conclusion: 结果表明量能器基强子鉴别（calorimeter-based hadron identification）具有令人鼓舞的基准性能，建议将其纳入未来高粒度量能器的优化目标之中。

Abstract: Highly granular calorimeters provide detailed information about hadronic-shower development that may enable particle identification beyond their conventional role in energy measurement. We investigate how well this information can distinguish protons from positively charged pions and how the achievable discrimination depends on detector segmentation and particle energy. The study uses Geant4 simulations of isolated particles with energies from 10 to 100 GeV in a homogeneous lead-tungstate calorimeter. A Deep Sets model operating directly on cell positions and detected energy and time outperforms a boosted decision tree based on reconstructed shower observables. With cells measuring $3 \times 3 \times 6$ mm$^3$, Deep Sets achieves an accuracy of 93.8% at 10 GeV, decreasing to 67.2% at 100 GeV. Shower topology is independently informative, deposited energy provides the largest additional contribution, and timing supplies complementary information. Coarser segmentation reduces discrimination, with performance more sensitive to longitudinal than transverse granularity. These results provide an encouraging benchmark for calorimeter-based hadron identification and motivate its inclusion among the optimization targets for future highly granular calorimeters.

</details>

### [111] [Spectral convergence of hyperbolic surfaces and quantum ergodicity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18730)
*Giacomo Gavelli*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 本文将Plancherel收敛（Plancherel convergence）的概念从局部紧群的紧商推广到有限面积双曲曲面（finite-area hyperbolic surfaces），并建立了Plancherel序列的量子遍历性（quantum ergodicity）定理，证明具有一致有界核与一致有界传播的积分算子的期望值在固定紧区间内的特征值上渐近等分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于退化双曲曲面上量子遍历性的研究（如Le Masson与Sahlsten关于乘法算子的工作）仅覆盖了传播界为零且核支持在对角线上的退化情形。本文旨在建立更一般的框架，允许核具有有限传播且系统无一致下界时仍能获得量子遍历性结果，从而将Plancherel收敛识别为退化双曲曲面上量子遍历性的自然谱框架。

Method: 通过将Plancherel收敛概念从局部紧群的紧商推广至有限面积双曲曲面，建立Plancherel序列的量子遍历性定理；该定理考察核一致有界且传播界一致有界的积分算子的期望值，证明其在固定紧区间内的特征值上渐近等分布。误差项与收敛性由Plancherel收敛条件约束，而非依赖于系统的均匀下界。

Result: 证明了Plancherel序列的量子遍历性定理：对核一致有界且传播一致有界的积分算子，其特征值的期望值在平均意义下渐近等分布。该结果推广了Le Masson和Sahlsten的乘法算子情形，且允许系统的systole（最短闭测地线长度）缩小而不需要一致下界，退化程度仅由Plancherel收敛性约束。

Conclusion: Plancherel收敛是退化双曲曲面上量子遍历性的自然谱框架，它超越了仅限对角核的乘法算子情形，为更一般的一致有界传播积分算子提供了统一的遍历性理论，同时允许几何退化受Plancherel收敛条件而非均匀下界的约束。

Abstract: We extend the notion of Plancherel convergence, introduced in the study of compact quotients of locally compact groups, to finite-area hyperbolic surfaces and establish a quantum ergodicity theorem for Plancherel sequences. We show that the expectation values of integral operators whose kernels are uniformly bounded and have uniformly bounded propagation become asymptotically equidistributed, on average, for eigenvalues in a fixed compact interval. Our result recovers the case of multiplication operators studied by Le Masson and Sahlsten, corresponding to the degenerate case of propagation bound zero and kernels supported on the diagonal. Moreover, our result allows the systole to shrink without a uniform lower bound, with the allowed degeneration constrained by Plancherel convergence. This identifies Plancherel convergence as a natural spectral framework for quantum ergodicity on degenerating hyperbolic surfaces.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.stat-mech'></div>

# cond-mat.stat-mech [[Back]](#toc)

### [112] [Embedding Paired Free-Fermion Gaussian States into Gutzwiller-Projected Bardeen--Cooper--Schrieffer Wave Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18168)
*Ryui Kaneko*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文以横向场伊辛模型（transverse-field Ising model）为例，精确地将任意可在选定的粒子基中表示为配对指数（paired exponential）的偶宇称无自旋费米高斯态嵌入到自旋 Abrikosov 费米子的投影 BCS 态中，为非可积模型的变分蒙特卡洛研究提供可控初始态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gutzwiller 投影的 Bardeen–Cooper–Schrieffer（BCS）波函数被广泛用于描述量子多体态，但如何系统地将一般高斯费米态嵌入到 Abrikosov 费米子的投影 BCS 态中仍缺乏精确的构造方法。

Method: 以一维横场伊辛模型为具体实例，作者证明了任意偶宇称的无自旋费米高斯态（若可在所选粒子基中表示为配对指数形式）均可精确嵌入到自旋 Abrikosov 费米子的投影 BCS 态中，从而建立了二者之间的严格对应关系。

Result: 该构造为变分蒙特卡洛（variational Monte Carlo）研究中非可积模型（nonintegrable models）的模拟提供了可控的初始态选择。

Conclusion: 这项工作建立了从一般高斯态到投影 BCS 态的系统嵌入机制，拓展了投影 BCS 波函数在非可积量子多体模型中的应用途径。

Abstract: Gutzwiller-projected Bardeen--Cooper--Schrieffer (BCS) wave functions of Abrikosov fermions are widely used to describe quantum many-body states. Taking the one-dimensional transverse-field Ising model as an example, we exactly embed any even-parity spinless fermionic Gaussian state representable as a paired exponential in the chosen particle basis into a projected BCS state of spinful Abrikosov fermions. The construction provides controlled initial states for variational Monte Carlo studies of nonintegrable models.

</details>
