### [113] [Order-Sensitive Quantum Distinguishability in Hawking Greybody Scattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18162)
*Nazir A. Ganaie*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了在黑洞灰体散射（greybody scattering）前后施加相位敏感高斯控制（phase-sensitive Gaussian control）时，复合顺序（composition order）如何在操作上变得可区分，并从精确的衰减-压缩响应中导出了区分度与灰体透射、占据数标度之间的红外指数传递定律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解黑洞散射过程中量子通道复合顺序的可区分性，是量子信息与引力交叉领域的重要问题。摘要指出，当相位敏感的高斯控制施加于灰体散射之前或之后时，需要建立操作层面上可区分的判据，并精确刻画这种区分度在红外（infrared）区域的标度行为。

Method: 作者将散射过程在标定的渐近模式端口（calibrated asymptotic mode ports）上形式化为玻色子衰减通道（bosonic attenuation channel），并将控制实现为标定的压缩变换（squeezing transformation）。在有限维忠实族（faithful families）中，利用局部对称化的Umegaki响应（symmetrized Umegaki response）及其与Bogoliubov-Kubo-Mori范数的联系进行刻画；在玻色子情形中，于高斯态（Gaussian state）层面精确求解衰减-压缩响应问题。

Result: 精确的衰减-压缩响应导出了红外指数传递定律（infrared exponent-transfer law），将灰体透射与占据数标度和可区分性关联起来。对于Schwarzschild背景上的无质量标量场，匹配低频散射给出固定的分波（partial-wave）对数系数和局部可积的包络响应。有限压缩下，红外输出趋近于互不相同的纯高斯支撑，产生有限的一阶以上的夹心Rényi散度（sandwiched Rényi divergence）、单位阶的对数Umegaki增长以及更高阶的发散。Schwarzschild-de Sitter边界方案产生受控的源隔离与乘积视界交叉扇区。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，所提出的方法适用于标定的渐近端口复合以及约化的有限带宽选择模式实现，并需满足所述窄带与通道假设，从而为黑洞散射中的量子通道顺序可区分性提供了精确判据。

Abstract: We study how composition order becomes operationally distinguishable when a phase-sensitive Gaussian control is applied before or after black-hole greybody scattering. The scattering process is formulated on calibrated asymptotic mode ports as a bosonic attenuation channel, and the control is a calibrated squeezing transformation. For finite-dimensional faithful families near a common faithful output, the local symmetrized Umegaki response is governed by the Bogoliubov-Kubo-Mori norm of the channel-composition defect; the corresponding bosonic problem is solved exactly at the Gaussian-state level. The exact attenuation-squeezing response yields an infrared exponent-transfer law relating greybody transmission and occupation scalings to distinguishability. For a massless scalar on Schwarzschild, matched low-frequency scattering gives an explicit fixed-partial-wave logarithmic coefficient and a locally integrable packet response. At finite squeezing, the infrared outputs approach distinct pure Gaussian supports, producing finite sandwiched Rényi divergence below unit order, logarithmic Umegaki growth at unit order, and divergence above unit order. Schwarzschild-de Sitter boundary prescriptions generate controlled source-isolated and product-horizon crossover sectors. The results apply to calibrated asymptotic-port compositions and to the reduced finite-band selected-mode realization under the stated narrowband and channel assumptions.

</details>


### [114] [A more general exact solution for the Schwarzschild black hole in a Bertotti-Robinson-Bonnor-Melvin universe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18213)
*Andrea Di Pinto*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文为Ovcharenko-Podolský解族引入新参数化，修正了原参数在负质量与热力学一致性上的病理问题，并通过Harrison变换（在Ernst方法框架内）扩展出包含Bonnor-Melvin型外部电磁场的新Einstein-Maxwell解族，最后考察了其真空子情形与已知度规的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始Ovcharenko-Podolský参数化在求解场方程时可能导致黑洞出现负质量，且存在与黑洞热力学定律不一致的病理问题，需要新的参数化来修正这些缺陷，并进一步将解推广到包含更一般类型的外部电磁场。

Method: 首先提出针对Ovcharenko-Podolský解族的新参数化，引入额外的规范常数（gauge constants）以消除病理问题并确保热力学一致性；随后在Ernst方法框架内通过Harrison变换（Harrison transformation）将该参数化推广，构造出包含Bonnor-Melvin型外部电磁场的新解族；最后分析其真空子情形并与已知度规建立联系。

Result: 新参数化在所有解的分支上均产生正质量黑洞，且引入的规范常数确保了与黑洞热力学定律的一致性；经Harrison变换获得的新Einstein-Maxwell解族同样满足热力学一致性要求，其真空子情形可建立与先前已知度规的对应关系。

Conclusion: 新参数化及推广后的解族为描述浸没在外部电磁场中的静态黑洞提供了在热力学上自洽的精确解，解决了原Ovcharenko-Podolský解的负质量与病理问题，并拓展了Bonnor-Melvin型外部场的适用场景。

Abstract: We begin by introducing a new parameterization for the Ovcharenko-Podolský family of exact solutions of the Einstein-Maxwell equations describing uncharged and non-accelerating static black holes immersed in an external Bertotti-Robinson electromagnetic field. The new spacetime is, in general, different from the original Ovcharenko-Podolský one, in particular on the branches solving the field equations, for which the original parameterization can lead to black holes with a negative mass, whereas the new one always leads to black holes with a positive mass. Moreover, by introducing additional gauge constants, we remove the various pathologies and ensure consistency with the laws of black hole thermodynamics. We then obtain a new family of Einstein-Maxwell solutions by generalizing this new parameterization, through Harrison transformation within the Ernst method, to also include an external electromagnetic field of the Bonnor-Melvin type. We again identify the appropriate gauge constants required to remove the possible pathologies and ensure that the resulting solutions satisfy the laws of black hole thermodynamics. Finally, we investigate the vacuum subcase of the new family and establish its relation to previously known metrics.

</details>


### [115] [Causal structure and light-conical singularities in Lorentzian simplicial quantum gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18216)
*Bianca Dittrich,José Padua-Argüelles*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了洛伦兹Regge引力中光锥奇异性（light-conical singularities）的普遍性与结构特征。作者开发了一种高效算法随机生成满足广义三角不等式的可实现Regge几何，并对四维三角剖分的构型空间进行采样。研究发现光锥奇异在共享多个四单纯形的骨（bone）上是普遍存在的，其中裤型（trouser-type）奇异性在熵意义上占优；对子单纯形因果性质的限制会显著改变这些统计特征。此外，作者引入成对可嵌入时序拓扑（pairwise embeddable chrono-topologies）和离散因果数据概念，进一步分析洛伦兹三角剖分的因果结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 洛伦兹引力路径积分的定义需要明确哪些因果结构在壳外（off shell）被允许，而光锥奇异（light-conical singularities）作为余维数为二的轻锥结构不规则性，会使引力作用量变为复值，从而导致构型的指数增强或抑制。理解这类奇异性在构型空间中的分布与频度，对于规范Regge引力、自旋泡沫模型（spin-foam models）及连续引力路径积分的因果条件至关重要。

Method: 作者开发了一种高效算法，用于随机生成满足广义三角不等式（generalized triangle inequalities）的可实现Regge几何，并将其应用于四维三角剖分构型空间的采样。通过统计采样分析光锥奇异的出现频率与类型分布，同时考察对子单纯形（如四面体须为类空）施加因果限制时统计特征的变化。此外，作者提出了成对可嵌入时序拓扑与离散因果数据等新概念，用于更一般地分析洛伦兹三角剖分的因果结构。

Result: 研究发现光锥奇异性在Regge构型空间中具有普遍性：对于共享多个四单纯形的骨，不规则轻锥结构的构型压倒性地占主导，其中裤型奇异性通常在熵上更为有利。如果要求所有四面体均为类空（spacelike），这些统计会被显著改变。进一步的，约穆尔克型（yarmulke-type）以及四维中的裤型奇异性可以出现在与简单细化移动（refinement moves）相关的无界构型区域上。

Conclusion: 因果不规则性是未受限制的洛伦兹Regge构型空间的内在且丰富的特征。这一结论明确了在Regge引力、自旋泡沫模型以及最终的连续引力路径积分中应施加何种因果条件这一关键问题，为后续理论约束的制定提供了定量基础。

Abstract: The definition of a Lorentzian gravitational path integral requires specifying which causal struc- tures are admitted off shell. Of particular importance are light-conical singularities, codimension- two irregularities of the light cone structure that render the gravitational action complex and can therefore lead to exponential enhancement or suppression of configurations. We investigate the prevalence and structure of such singularities in Lorentzian Regge gravity. We develop an efficient algorithm for randomly generating realizable Regge geometries satisfying the generalized triangle inequalities, and use it to sample the configuration space of four-dimensional triangulations. We find that light-conical singularities are generic: for bones shared by many four-simplices, configurations with irregular light cone structure overwhelmingly dominate, with trouser-type singularities gen- erally entropically favored. Restrictions on the causal character of subsimplices, such as requiring all tetrahedra to be spacelike, can substantially alter these statistics. We further show that both yarmulke- and, in four dimensions, trouser-type singularities can occur on unbounded regions of configuration space associated with simple refinement moves. Beyond light-conical singularities, we analyze the causal structure of Lorentzian triangulations more generally, for example by introducing the notion of pairwise embeddable chrono-topologies and a set of discrete causality data. Our re- sults demonstrate that causal irregularities are an intrinsic and abundant feature of the unrestricted Lorentzian Regge configuration space, sharpening the question of which causality conditions should be imposed in Regge gravity, spin-foam models, and ultimately the continuum gravitational path integral.

</details>


### [116] [No parametrisation, No Problem: A Weakly Modelled Framework to Constrain the Luminosity Distance--Redshift Relation Using Gravitational Wave Sirens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18310)
*Elena Colangeli,Konstantin Leyde,Tessa Baker,Anson Chen*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种准模型无关（quasi-model-independent）框架，用于通过引力波（gravitational waves, GWs）数据重构引力波与标准电磁光度距离之比的广义相对论（general relativity, GR）偏差。将该框架应用于GWTC-5数据未发现GR偏差，但模拟显示第三代探测器可自信检测约5%量级、3σ显著性的偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有宇宙学检验GR的方法通常依赖于对光度距离-红移（$d_{\rm L}-z$）关系的参数化形式，但这类参数化仅涵盖有限的引力修正行为，难以代表完整的修正引力理论（modified gravity theories）多样性。因此需要一个能覆盖更广泛偏差特征、且不会引入模型偏差的重构方法。

Method: 提出一个准模型无关框架，直接重构引力波到电磁光度距离比值中相对GR的偏差。该框架不预设特定的参数化偏差形式，能同时约束单调（monotonic）、不规则（irregular）和振荡（oscillatory）特征，且不会使总体分布（population distribution）参数产生偏差。

Result: 将框架应用于GWTC-5数据，未检测到对GR的偏差。模拟表明，O5类引力波数据无法分辨小于30%的GR偏差；而第三代引力波探测器能够以3σ显著性自信检测约5%量级的偏差。

Conclusion: 该准模型无关框架为检验修正引力理论提供了比传统参数化方法更广泛的覆盖能力，现阶段的GWTC-5数据与GR一致。未来第三代探测器有望将GR偏差的检测灵敏度提升至5%量级。

Abstract: Cosmological tests of general relativity (GR) using gravitational waves (GWs) often rely on parametrised forms of the luminosity distance-redshift ($ d_{\rm L} - z$) relation, which is modified in alternative theories of gravity where the gravitational coupling strength is time-dependent. Although they can lead to stringent results, these parametrisations encapsulate a restricted range of behaviours that may not represent the full variety of modified gravity theories. We present a quasi-model-independent framework to reconstruct deviations from GR in the ratio of the GW to standard electromagnetic luminosity distance. We find that our method can confidently constrain monotonic, irregular, and oscillatory features in the ratio, without biasing population distribution parameters. Applying our framework to GWTC-5 data, we find no deviations from GR. We report that O5-like GW data will not resolve deviations from GR smaller than 30% with our method; however, third generation GW detectors can confidently detect deviations of order 5% to 3$σ$ significance.

</details>


### [117] [Curvature-induced phantom behavior and cosmological bounces without violating the Dominant Energy Condition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18313)
*Miguel Cruz,Samuel Lepe*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文证明，在空间闭合（spatially closed）的宇宙中，即使物质严格满足主能量条件（Dominant Energy Condition, DEC），正的空间曲率本身也能驱动减速参数降至 $q<-1$，产生类似幻影（phantom）的有效动力学，并在有限时间内使宇宙膨胀停止于有限尺度因子处，该事件被解释为宇宙反弹（cosmological bounce）而非破坏性奇点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准幻影宇宙模型（phantom cosmology）以幻影流体驱动大撕裂（Big Rip）奇点，但这违反了能量条件。作者旨在探索是否存在一种不违反主能量条件即可产生幻影有效动力学的替代机制，并考察空间曲率在这一过程中的作用。

Method: 作者在弗里德曼-勒梅特-罗伯逊-沃尔克（Friedmann-Lemaître-Robertson-Walker）框架下，考虑具有非零空间曲率的闭合宇宙几何，分析满足 $ω\ge-1$ 的物质在正空间曲率作用下对减速参数和哈勃参数（Hubble parameter）演化行为的影响，并与真正的幻影流体（$ω<-1$）情形进行对比。

Result: 对于遵守主能量条件的物质（$ω\ge-1$），正空间曲率可将减速参数驱动至低于 $-1$ 的数值，使膨胀在有限尺度因子处停止，此时哈勃参数为零而能量密度保持有限；由于曲率同时使哈勃半径保持正则，该事件被自然解释为宇宙反弹而非破坏性奇点。在真正幻影情形（$ω<-1$）下，相同闭合几何会先产生早期反弹，随后走向晚期大撕裂。

Conclusion: 空间曲率可作为主动的几何贡献者，在满足主能量条件的闭合宇宙中产生有效的幻影动力学和无奇点的有限时间宇宙事件。文章还评论了曲率贡献的观测现状。

Abstract: We show that a spatially closed universe can exhibit effective phantom dynamics ($q<-1$) and reach a finite-time cosmological event without violating the Dominant Energy Condition (DEC). Whereas the standard Big Rip is driven by a phantom fluid that breaks the energy conditions, here non-null spatial curvature acts as an active geometric contributor: for matter that strictly obeys the DEC ($ω\ge-1$), positive spatial curvature by itself drives the deceleration parameter to values below $-1$ and brings the expansion to a stop at a finite value of the scale factor, where the Hubble parameter vanishes while the energy density stays finite. Since the curvature also keeps the Hubble radius regular at this point, the event is naturally interpreted as a cosmological bounce rather than a disruptive singularity. We contrast this DEC-preserving mechanism with the genuine phantom case ($ω<-1$), for which the same closed geometry produces an early bounce followed by a late Big Rip, and we comment on the observational status of the curvature contribution.

</details>


### [118] [Chromatic Weak Lensing by Charged Black Holes with Two Lorentz-Violating Kalb-Ramond Couplings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18315)
*Ali Övgün,Reggie C. Pantig,Grigoris Panotopoulos*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究洛伦兹破坏（Lorentz-violating）的Kalb-Ramond背景中静态带电黑洞的弱引力透镜与稳态球形测试流体吸积，通过Gauss-Bonnet定理得到二阶后闵可夫斯基展开的完整局部偏转角，并区分了质量、电荷、两种非最小耦合及均匀冷等离子体中的色散效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 洛伦兹破坏背景下的黑洞物理是检验基础物理的重要途径，但该框架下的引力透镜与吸积过程尚未被系统解析刻画，特别需要厘清不同耦合参数对可观测效应的独立贡献。

Method: 采用Gauss-Bonnet定理（Gauss-Bonnet theorem）并引入正确的边界项与扰动光线边界，计算至二阶后闵可夫斯基（post-Minkowskian）阶的完整局部偏转角；同时假设测试辐射为最小耦合探针，并在均匀冷等离子体（cold plasma）中分析频率依赖；对中性绝热吸积，推导守恒通量、Bernoulli关系、Hamiltonian流及声点（sonic-point）条件。

Result: 在均匀冷等离子体中，质量与电荷扇区呈现不同的频率依赖，产生可区分的色散特征；两种洛伦兹破坏耦合被分离：一种控制锥形几何（conical geometry）与质量归一化，另一种首先通过有效电荷（effective charge）进入。中性绝热吸积的守恒量、Bernoulli关系、Hamiltonian流与声点条件均已解析导出。

Conclusion: 上述结果将局部、全局、色散与吸积效应相互解耦，并建立用于唯象约束（phenomenological constraints）所需的标定关系，为后续观测检验该洛伦兹破坏背景提供理论基础。

Abstract: We study weak gravitational lensing and steady spherical test--fluid accretion by a static charged black hole in a Lorentz--violating Kalb--Ramond background with two nonminimal curvature couplings, assuming minimally coupled probe radiation. Using the Gauss--Bonnet theorem with the correct boundary term and perturbed ray boundary, we obtain the complete local deflection angle through second post--Minkowskian order. In a homogeneous cold plasma, the mass and charge sectors acquire different frequency dependences, producing distinct chromatic signatures. The two Lorentz--violating couplings are also separated: one controls the conical geometry and mass normalization, while the other first enters through the effective charge. For neutral adiabatic accretion, we derive the conserved fluxes, Bernoulli relation, Hamiltonian flow, and sonic--point conditions. These results disentangle local, global, dispersive, and accretion effects and establish the calibrations required for phenomenological constraints.

</details>


### [119] [Bounds on the Lyapunov Exponent of Circular Null Orbits in $n$-Dimensional Black-Hole Spacetimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18436)
*Anagha V.,C. Fairoos,T. K. Safir*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了任意维度爱因斯坦引力下静态球对称渐近平坦黑洞的光子球不稳定性上界，证明了在切线零能量条件下，李雅普诺夫指数存在与维度相关的上界，并推广了已知的四维结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在四维爱因斯坦引力中，不稳定圆零轨道的失稳率受模型无关上界约束，本文旨在将该分析推广至任意维度并考虑各向异性物质分布，以建立维度与光子捕获不稳定性之间的统一联系。

Method: 作者在任意维度爱因斯坦引力框架下，针对静态球对称渐近平坦黑洞并允许各向异性物质分布，利用切线零能量条件推导李雅普诺夫指数的维度依赖上界，并以一般化表面引力和度规函数在光子球处取值表达该界，同时推导了与临界碰撞参数（阴影半径）、轨道频率、局域加速度标度及准正则模阻尼相关的系列不等式。

Result: 对于n维黑洞时空，李雅普诺夫指数满足上界λ≤√(n-3)·κ_γ/√(μ_γ)；所有主要不等式在n=4时退化为已知的四维结果；涉及临界碰撞参数和轨道频率的上界被Schwarzschild-Tangherlini真空解饱和。

Conclusion: 这些结果表明，维度信息可作为一致性的约束条件，将时空维度与爱因斯坦引力中光子捕获的不稳定性联系起来，为黑洞光学、光子环、强引力透镜和准正则模振荡的几何联系提供了维度推广的紧致框架。

Abstract: Unstable circular null orbits provide a geometric bridge between black-hole optics, photon rings, strong gravitational lensing, and the eikonal sector of quasinormal ringing. In four-dimensional Einstein gravity, the instability rate of such orbits, measured by the Lyapunov exponent $λ$, obeys model-independent upper bounds when the matter sector satisfies standard energy conditions. We extend this analysis to static, spherically symmetric, asymptotically flat black holes in arbitrary dimensional Einstein gravity, allowing for an anisotropic matter distribution. We show that, under the tangential null energy condition, the Lyapunov exponent admits a dimension-dependent upper bound in terms of the generalized surface gravity $κ(r)$ and the metric function $μ(r)$, both evaluated at the photon sphere $r =r_γ$. For $n$- dimensional black hole spacetime, the bound is $λ\leq\sqrt{n-3}\, {κ_γ}/{\sqrt{μ_γ}}$. Related bounds are derived in terms of the critical impact parameter (shadow radius), the orbital frequency, a local acceleration scale, and eikonal quasinormal-mode damping. All principal inequalities reduce to the known four-dimensional results for $n=4$. Also, the bounds involving the critical impact parameter and orbital frequency are saturated by the Schwarzschild-Tangherlini vacuum solution. The results provide a compact set of consistency conditions linking the dimensionality of spacetime to the instability of photon trapping in Einstein gravity.

</details>


### [120] [Covariant Energy Momentum Tensor in General Relativity by Generalized Canonical Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18459)
*Masakatsu Kenmoku*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文针对广义相对论中引力能量-动量张量的协变定义问题，提出了一种在广义正则形式下的完整协变定义方法。其核心思想是将度规张量的高阶微分项纳入引力作用量，从而在任何坐标系（包括极坐标系和渐近洛伦兹系统）中一致地获得正确的引力能量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在广义相对论（general relativity）中定义一个完整且协变的能量-动量张量（energy-momentum tensor）是一个长期未解决的难题。传统上广泛采用的方法是在渐近洛伦兹系（asymptotic Lorentz system）中将引力能量-动量张量定义为伪张量（pseudo-tensor），但这在任意坐标系中缺乏协变性。

Method: 该方法的关键在于将度规张量（metric tensor）的高阶微分项纳入引力作用量（gravitational action）中，以一致地推导出能量-动量张量。通过这种处理，所得到的能量-动量张量具有完全协变的形式，在广义正则形式（generalized canonical form）下给出结果。

Result: 所得引力能量-动量张量具有完全协变的形式，确保在任何坐标系中都能正确获得引力能量，包括极坐标系（polar coordinates）和渐近洛伦兹系统。

Conclusion: 该工作为广义相对论中引力能量-动量张量的协变定义提供了一个完整且一致的方案，解决了传统伪张量方法在一般坐标系下缺乏协变性的问题。

Abstract: Defining a complete and covariant energy-momentum tensor in general relativity is a longstanding issue of concern. A widely attempted approach is to define it as a pseudo-tensor in an asymptotic Lorentz system. In this paper, we present a complete covariant definition of the gravitational energy-momentum tensor in the generalized canonical form.
  The key is incorporate higher-order differential terms of the metric tensor into the gravitational action in order to derive the energy-momentum tensor consistently. This completely covariant form ensures that the gravitational energy is correctly obtained in any coordinate system, including polar coordinates and asymptotic Lorentz systems.

</details>


### [121] [On uniqueness of non-vacuum stationary axisymmetric type D spacetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18653)
*Hiroaki Nakajima,Ya Guo,Wenbin Lin*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了一族非真空稳态轴对称D型度规（type D metrics），证明了共形到Carter度规（conformal-to-Carter metric）是该族的最一般形式，且无需之前Ovcharenko与Podolský所用假设即可得出此结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前Ovcharenko与Podolský在推导该结论时依赖于若干附加假设，本文旨在不加这些假设的情况下，直接证明共形到Carter度规是非真空稳态轴对称D型度规族的最一般形式。

Method: 研究对象为两个主零方向（principal null directions）均为测地且无剪切（geodesic and shearfree）的非真空稳态轴对称类型D度规族，通过分析其几何结构确立共形到Carter度规的普遍性。

Result: 证明了共形到Carter度规是该度规族的最一般形式，且该结论的成立不依赖此前研究所使用的任何额外假设。

Conclusion: 共形到Carter度规具有更广泛的适用性，统一并推广了此前对非真空稳态轴对称D型度规族的刻画结果。

Abstract: We study a family of the non-vacuum stationary axisymmetric type D metrics, where the two principal null directions are geodesic and shearfree. It is demonstrated that the conformal-to-Carter metric gives the most general form of this family, without any assumptions which are previously used by Ovcharenko and Podolský to arrive at this conclusion.

</details>


### [122] [First Law of Black Hole Interior Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18583)
*Ze-Xuan Xiong,H. Lu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该论文在事件视界（event horizon）与类Kasner奇点（Kasner-like singularity）之间的宇宙学解所支配的内部几何中，建立了一个局域动力学第一定律。通过评估Iyer-Wald恒等式，定义了Kasner势和输运响应系数，并用精确解与数值解验证了该第一定律，为研究无需外部几何与渐近荷纠缠的内部动力学提供了自洽框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文试图解决黑洞内部几何中动力学描述缺乏局部第一定律形式的问题，此前相关研究往往需要依赖外部几何与渐近荷（asymptotic charges）来刻画内部结构，本文希望建立一种自洽方法，避免这种依赖，并可能为内部动力学的相结构研究提供新途径。

Method: 作者在由事件视界与类Kasner奇点连接起来的宇宙学解所描绘的内部几何中，评估其间的Iyer-Wald恒等式，据此定义了一个Kasner势（Kasner potential）和输运响应系数（transported response coefficients），进而构造局域动力学第一定律，并通过精确解与数值解对其进行验证。

Result: 研究得到了一个局域第一定律形式的动力学关系，其有效性经由精确解与数值解的双重检验；由此建立的自洽框架无需诉诸外部几何与渐近荷即可研究内部动力学。

Conclusion: 该形式框架为研究黑洞内部动力学提供了一种自洽的新方法，不依赖外部几何与渐近荷，并可能为内部动力学的相结构（phase structures）带来新的研究视角。

Abstract: We obtain a local first law of dynamics within the interior geometries that are governed by cosmological solutions connecting the event horizon and the Kasner-like singularity. Evaluating the Iyer-Wald identity between the horizon and near-singularity geometries, we define a Kasner potential and transported response coefficients. We test the first law by both exact and numerical solutions. Our formalism provides a self-consistent approach to study the interior dynamics without having to appeal to the outside geometry and the asymptotic charges. It may provide a new approach to study the phase structures of the interior dynamics.

</details>


### [123] [Effective curvature coupling of link-based causal set propagators in $1+1$ dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18753)
*Arsim Kastrati*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究因果集（causal set）中基于链路（link）定义的传播子在弯曲时空中的响应，发现体积依赖会产生领先阶曲率修正，对应于有效曲率耦合ξR，耦合常数ξ_eff=1/6（即有效质量平方m²=ξ_eff R），这一耦合并非人为引入，而是从基于链路的路径求和本身涌现，并得到AdS₁₊₁和dS₁₊₁数值模拟的支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在平直1+1维闵可夫斯基空间（Minkowski space）的撒点（sprinkling）上，因果集传播子已能在大尺度上重现无质量延迟连续格林函数，但该传播子如何响应曲率尚不清楚。理解因果集传播子在弯曲时空中的行为，是检验因果集理论能否在宏观上恢复连续时空物理学的关键一步。

Method: 作者分析了共形平坦嵌入中、具有常曲率R的因果集传播子。由于共形平坦时空的因果序不变，但链接关系依赖于所张开的因果菱形（causal diamond）的物理体积，作者通过分析该体积依赖推导传播子的领先曲率修正，并将其与连续标量传播子的有效曲率耦合⁠ξR进行比较。同时，在AdS₁₊₁和dS₁₊₁嵌入的撒点上进行数值模拟以验证理论预测。

Result: 在大距离上，领先曲率修正等价于具有有效曲率耦合ξR的连续标量传播子，耦合常数ξ_eff=1/6，对应于有效质量平方参数m²_eff=ξ_eff R。该耦合并非人工插入，而是从基于链路的路径求和（path sum）中自然涌现。数值模拟支持所预测的曲率响应，并表明随着撒点密度增加该有效耦合持续存在。

Conclusion: 基于链路的因果集传播子自然产生1/6的有效曲率耦合，反映了因果集微观结构对宏观传播子行为的影响。这一结果为因果集理论在弯曲时空中的一致性提供了支持，并表明其微观物理可以在连续极限下涌现出特定的曲率修正项。

Abstract: We study how a recently introduced causal-set propagator defined in terms of links responds to curvature. On sprinklings embedded in a flat 1+1-dimensional Minkowski space, this propagator is known to reproduce the massless retarded continuum Greens function on large scales. In conformally flat embeddings with constant curvature $\mathcal R$, the causal order is unchanged, but the link relationship is sensitive to the physical volume of the spanned causal diamond. We show that this volume dependence generates a leading curvature correction to the propagator. On large distances, this correction is equivalent to a continuum scalar propagator with an effective curvature coupling of the form $ξ\mathcal R$ with the coupling constant $ξ_{\rm eff}=1/6$, equivalently corresponding to an effective mass-squared parameter $m_{\rm eff}^2=ξ_{\rm eff}\mathcal R$. This coupling is not inserted by hand but emerges from the link-based path sum itself, reflecting the microscopic structure of the causal set. Numerical simulations on sprinklings embedded in $\mathrm{AdS}_{1+1}$ and $\mathrm{dS}_{1+1}$ support the predicted curvature response and indicate that the effective coupling persists as the sprinkling density is increased.

</details>


### [124] [A Quantum-Gravity-Motivated GUP Effective Metric](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18807)
*M. H. Al Ghifari,M. F. Fauzi,A. Rohim,H. S. Ramadhan,H. Alatas,A. Sulaksono*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种尺度依赖的二次广义不确定原理（GUP）有效度规，通过引入插值函数Δp(r)改进动量依赖度规，使该度规在短程和长程上准确捕获Δp(r)的不同行为，并与重整化群（RG）理论导出的度规结构一致，但RG参数γ̂可关联到无量纲GUP参数β₀，从而区别于RG方法。该模型在热力学和黑洞阴影预测上优于启发式方法及其他GUP有效度规。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期批评（Ong 2023）指出了广义不确定原理（GUP）有效度规的某些问题，需要改进现有构建方法。相较于Xiang等人（2018）的流程，动量依赖度规在短程和长程上未能准确捕获Δp(r)的区分行为，亟需改善。

Method: 通过分析引力诱导相移（COW实验）和爱因斯坦-玻尔光子箱思想实验（Gedanken experiment）构建尺度依赖的二次GUP有效度规。引入插值函数Δp(r)，采用有效距离（effective distance）概念以准确描述Δp(r)在短程与长程区域的不同行为，对Xiang等人（2018）的动量依赖度规进行改进。

Result: 所得有效度规与重整化群（Renormalization Group, RG）理论导出的度规结构相同，但RG参数γ̂可关联到无量纲GUP参数β₀。模型预测表现出内部一致性，在特定极限下与若干量子黑洞模型的现象学预测相符。相比启发式方法和已有的GUP有效度规，该度规改善了黑洞热力学（thermodynamics）和阴影（shadow）预测。

Conclusion: 该尺度依赖GUP有效度规在结构上与RG方法一致但参数来源不同，具备模型内部一致性和与量子黑洞模型的相容性，且在黑洞热力学与阴影预测上表现更优。此外，GUP与f(R)引力之间的关系（D'Agostino等人，2026）可能为GUP的更基础描述提供未来路径。

Abstract: Recent critiques have addressed certain aspects of the generalized uncertainty principle (GUP) effective metric (Ong 2023). This study presents a scale-dependent quadratic GUP effective metric, constructed through analyses of the gravity-induced phase shift (COW experiment) and the Einstein-Bohr photon box Gedanken experiment. In contrast to the procedure outlined in (Xiang et al. 2018), the momentum-dependent metric is improved by introducing an interpolating function $Δp (r)$, which employs the effective distance concept to accurately capture the distinct behavior of $Δp (r)$ in both short and long distance regimes. The resulting effective metric exhibits the same structure as that derived from the Renormalization Group (RG) theory. However, the RG parameter $\hatγ$ can now be related to the dimensionless GUP parameter $β_0$, thereby distinguishing this metric from the RG-based approach. The corresponding effective metric prediction demonstrates internal consistency of the model, and phenomenologically the predictions are in agreement with some quantum black hole models in some limits. The effective metric improved black hole thermodynamics and shadow predictions compared to the heuristic approach and other proposed GUP effective metrics. Furthermore, the relationship between GUP and $f(R)$ gravity (D'Agostino et al. 2026) may provide a possible future route toward a more fundamental description of GUP.

</details>


### [125] [Duru-Kleinert Path Integral in Unimodular Quantum Cosmology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18983)
*Xiao-Kan Guo*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文使用Duru-Kleinert路径积分技术重新审视单形引力（unimodular gravity）量子宇宙学与氢原子之间的对应关系，将平坦FLRW宇宙的欧氏路径积分变换为一维库仑系统的路径积分，通过Mellin-Barnes积分表示提取束缚态极点以获得量子化的负宇宙学常数，并计算从“无”到宇宙的量子隧穿率，以及正宇宙学常数情形下的谱密度和Krylov复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期研究表明，在单形引力框架下，充满无压尘埃和宇宙学常数的平坦FLRW宇宙的Wheeler-DeWitt方程等价于氢原子的径向薛定谔方程。作者旨在通过Duru-Kleinert路径积分技术重新考察这一对应关系，以提供新的计算视角并深入理解量子宇宙学的相关性质。

Method: 采用Duru-Kleinert时间重参数化技术（time reparametrization），将宇宙的欧氏路径积分变换为一维库仑系统（one-dimensional Coulomb system）的路径积分；通过Mellin-Barnes积分表示提取格林函数（Green's function）的束缚态极点；在库仑模型内计算宇宙从“无”诞生的量子隧穿率；对正宇宙学常数情形计算无界态的谱密度（spectral density）和Krylov复杂度（Krylov complexity）。

Result: 束缚态极点给出了量子化的负宇宙学常数；在库仑模型框架内直接计算了从“无”到宇宙的量子隧穿率；对于正宇宙学常数，获得了无界态的谱密度和Krylov复杂度的结果。

Conclusion: Duru-Kleinert路径积分技术为单形量子宇宙学与氢原子之间的对应关系提供了系统的计算框架，使得量子隧穿率、谱密度与Krylov复杂度等物理量能够在该库仑模型中直接计算，深化了对量子宇宙起源的理解。

Abstract: The Wheeler-DeWitt equation of a flat Friedmann-Lemaitre-Robertson-Walker universe filled with pressureless dust and a cosmological constant in the unimodular gravity is recently shown to be equivalent to the radial Schrödinger equation of a hydrogen atom. In this paper, we revisit this correspondence between the unimodular quantum cosmology and the hydrogen atom by using the Duru-Kleinert path integration technique. The Duru-Kleinert time reparametrization transforms the Euclidean path integral of the universe into that of a one-dimensional Coulomb system. Through a Mellin-Barnes integral representation, we extract the bound state poles of the Green's function, which give the quantized negative cosmological constant. We compute the quantum tunneling rate from ``nothing'' to a universe directly within the Coulomb model. Furthermore, for a positive cosmological constant, we compute the spectral density and the Krylov complexity for the unbounded state.

</details>


### [126] [Notes on Kerr-Bertotti-Robinson Spacetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19136)
*Yu-Sen Zhou,Liang-Bi Wu,Ming-Fei Ji,Wen-Tao Fu,Li-Ming Cao,Rong-Gen Cai*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文发现Kerr–Bertotti–Robinson时空的r=∞表面并非真实边界，通过构造跨此表面的自然延拓生成由虫洞桥连接的多区域无限链，并研究该扩展几何中无质量标量场的拟正规模（quasinormal modes），证明存在纯虚不稳定的拟正规模、黑洞炸弹机制驱动的不稳定分支及弱阻尼的双势垒腔QNM，暗示回波响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KBR时空的r=∞表面并非无限延展光线的端点集合，且引力场强度的库仑型分量Ψ₂在该处仍不为零，表明此表面不是真实边界；此外，延拓暴露出无事件视界遮蔽的近邻环奇异性，对弱宇宙监督猜想构成挑战，因此需要研究扩展几何在扰动下是否稳定。

Method: 在KBR时空的r=∞表面上构造自然延拓，将相邻KBR区域的内外空间通过虫洞状桥梁连接并迭代生成无限链；随后在两宇宙散射段上研究测试无质量标量场的拟正规模，包括轴对称扰动的解析分析与(ℓ,m)=(2,2)扇区中的数值/半解析研究，并分析虫洞几何形成的双势垒腔结构。

Result: 对轴对称扰动，在每一个ℓ下均解析证明存在源于违反时间性区域（chronology-violating region）的纯虚不稳定QNM；在(ℓ,m)=(2,2)扇区发现由黑洞炸弹（black-hole bomb）机制驱动的不稳定QNM分支；虫洞几何产生双势垒腔及一族弱阻尼QNM，暗示回波状响应。

Conclusion: KBR时空的扩展几何在扰动下是不稳定的，其不稳定模式的来源与违反时间性区域及黑洞炸弹机制密切相关，同时虫洞结构支持的回波式QNM谱为引力波观测提供了潜在的可检验特征。

Abstract: The surface $r=\infty$ of the Kerr--Bertotti--Robinson (KBR) spacetime is not the collection of the endpoints of infinitely extended light rays, and the Coulomb type component of gravitational field strength represented by $Ψ_2$ remains nonvanishing there, indicating that this surface is not a real boundary. We construct a natural extension across this surface, which connects the exterior of one KBR region to the interior of a neighboring one and, upon iteration, produces an infinite chain of regions connected by wormhole-like bridges. The extension also exposes the neighboring ring singularity without an intervening horizon, challenging the weak cosmic censorship conjecture and raising the question of whether the extended geometry is stable under perturbations. We therefore study the quasinormal modes (QNMs) of a test massless scalar field on a two-universe scattering segment. For axisymmetric perturbations, the existence of purely imaginary unstable QNMs is analytically proved for every $\ell$, with their origin tied to the chronology-violating region. In the $(\ell,m)=(2,2)$ sector, unstable QNM branches driven by a black-hole-bomb mechanism are found. Finally, the wormhole geometry produces a double-barrier cavity and families of weakly damped QNMs, suggesting echo-like responses.

</details>


### [127] [On entropic cosmology, late time acceleration and the quantum bounce](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19146)
*Luis Rey Diaz-Barron,G. E. Pérez-Cuéllar,M. Sabido*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出一种统一的熵起源（entropic origin）描述，将圈量子宇宙学（Loop Quantum Cosmology, LQG）中的量子反弹与驱动宇宙晚期加速的暗能量（dark energy）统一起来。通过引入宇宙最小面积$A_0$以及由$S_Q$、$S_{BH}$和$S_{DE}$构成的修正熵面积，推导出弗里德曼方程（Friedmann equations）。在$A\sim A_0$极限下出现依赖于最小面积的临界密度并产生量子反弹；在$A\gg A_0$（大宇宙）极限下则得到加速膨胀的尺度因子，从而论证量子反弹与暗能量均具有熵起源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 圈量子宇宙学中的量子反弹与晚期宇宙加速膨胀的暗能量通常被视为互不相关的物理现象，本文试图从统一的热力学/熵视角为二者提供一致的理论基础。

Method: 作者假设宇宙具有最小面积$A_0$，并把熵面积构造为热力学熵$S_Q$、黑洞熵$S_{BH}$和暗能量熵$S_{DE}$之和，由此推导修改后的弗里德曼方程，进而在两个不同的极限（$A\approx A_0$与$A\gg A_0$）下分析宇宙动力学行为。

Result: 在$A\sim A_0$极限下，弗里德曼方程产生依赖于最小面积的临界密度，从而引发量子反弹；在$A\gg A_0$极限下，方程给出加速膨胀的尺度因子，对应宇宙晚期暗能量主导的加速阶段。

Conclusion: 量子反弹与暗能量都可归因于统一的熵起源机制，表明两者可能存在同一热力学原理下的内在联系。

Abstract: In this work, we present a unified entropic description of the quantum bounce that is present in Loop Quantum Cosmology (LQG) and dark energy that drives the late time acceleration of the Universe. By considering a minimal area $A_0$ for the Universe and a modified entropy area constructed as the sum of $S_Q$, $S_{BH}$ and $S_{DE}$, we derive the Friedmann equations. We show that in the limit $A\sim A_0$, there is a critical density that generates the quantum bounce and depends on the minimal area. Moreover, in the limit $A\gg A_0$ (large Universe) the late time dynamics gives an accelerating scale factor. Therefore, we can argue an entropic origin for the quantum bounce and dark energy.

</details>

### [128] [On the electronic and vibrational dimensionality of nanometer-scale silicon structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18277)
*Massimo V. Fischetti,Dallin O. Nielsen,Edward Chen*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文探讨半导体纳米结构中电子和振动（声子）维度的判定问题，提出以电子/声子的相干长度（inelastic mean free path）作为重要的长度尺度标准。以室温下的硅纳米片为例，估计电子经受量子限域（quantum confinement）的临界长度约为8 nm或更小，而声子由于不同散射机制，其限域长度尺度从约10 nm到数微米不等。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半导体纳米结构需满足怎样的厚度/宽度条件才能引发电子和声子限域，是纳米电子学与热输运领域的基础问题。文献中广泛采用的经验判据缺乏清晰的物理依据，作者旨在厘清这些判据背后的物理动机，并探讨何种长度尺度才是决定限域是否发生的关键。

Method: 作者将电子相干长度（定义为电子和声子的非弹性平均自由程）视为其“视野”（field of view），主张该长度即决定限域发生的关键长度尺度。基于此假设，结合文献中已有的数据，对室温下Si纳米片的电子与声子限域临界尺寸进行估算。

Result: 对于室温下硅纳米片，当相干长度由声子及栅控结构中远程声子（remote phonon）的能量损失决定时，电子经受量子限域的临界长度约为8 nm或更小。对于声子，其相干长度取决于与电子的散射及非谐三声子过程：短波长声学与光学声子仅在结构小至约10 nm时才可能被限域；而长波长声学声子在相干长度可达1微米量级，因而可在更大距离上被限域。

Conclusion: 电子与声子限域不存在统一的临界长度；电子限域的临界尺度由相干长度决定（约为8 nm量级），而声子限域取决于声子类型，尺度跨度可达三个数量级（从约10 nm到1微米）。作者明确此观点带有推测（speculate）性质。

Abstract: We discuss the problem of assessing the electronic and vibrational dimensionality of a semiconductor nanostructure: How thin and/or wide must a nanostructure be in order to induce electron and phonon confinement? Clarifying the physical justification for common criteria found in the literature, we view the electron coherence length (defined as the electron and phonon inelastic mean free path) as their `field of view' and argue (or, better yet, `speculate') that this sets the important length scale. Considering the example of Si nanosheets at room temperature, and drawing from results found in the literature, we estimate that the critical length below which electrons are subject to quantum confinement is of the order of (or smaller than) 8 nm, when their coherence length is determined by energy losses to phonons and remote phonons in gated structures. On the contrary, no single length-scale can be given for phonons: Taking their coherence length as determined by scattering with electrons and anharmonic three-phonon processes, short wavelength acoustic and optical phonons may be confined only by structures as small as 10 nm. Long-wavelength acoustic phonons, instead, may exhibit a coherence length of the order of 1 micrometer, so that they may be confined over much larger distances.

</details>


### [129] [Optical Voltage Profiling of 2D Semiconductors via Proximal Exciton Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18382)
*Ha-Leem Kim,Hyungbin Lim,Yuanyi Yang,Ruishi Qi,Ruichen Xia,Can Uzundal,Takashi Taniguchi,Kenji Watanabe,Feng Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种称为光学电压剖面测量（optical voltage profiling）的非侵入式全场技术，利用邻近的单层MoSe₂激子传感器替代局域电压探针，在接触电阻（contact resistance）极高的条件下定量测量半导体器件的二维电压分布，并以此研究了双层MoSe₂中由载流子密度驱动的金属-绝缘体转变（metal-insulator transition），揭示了输运中的空间异质性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原子级薄半导体器件中的高接触电阻往往掩盖其本征电输运性质，尤其在低载流子密度区域，而经典的局域电压探针方法难以克服这一限制。因此需要一种非侵入式的全场技术，能够在高接触电阻条件下解析器件内部的真实输运行为。

Method: 该方法采用光学电压剖面测量技术，以由薄层六方氮化硼（hexagonal boron nitride）隔离的近邻单层MoSe₂激子作为传感器，将目标器件的局域静电势转换为激子反射率（exciton reflectance）的空间分辨调制信号；通过逐像素原位标定（pixel-wise in situ calibration），可将这些信号转化为有源偏置半导体器件的定量二维电压图。

Result: 利用该方法，在金属区域尽管两端电阻（two-terminal resistance）高达兆欧量级，仍测得通道电阻（channel resistance）低至1 kΩ以下；光学导出的电阻在电阻量子h/e²附近表现出金属-绝缘体交叉（metal-insulator crossover），且电压图和重建的局域电导率均显示出绝缘态与金属态中显著的空间异质性。

Conclusion: 该技术不仅能在高接触电阻条件下解析通道电阻，还提供了对功能化范德华（van der Waals）器件中微观输运异质性（transport heterogeneity）的空间分辨测量途径，为研究低维半导体本征电子态开辟了新手段。

Abstract: High contact resistances in atomically thin semiconductors often mask intrinsic electrical transport properties, particularly at low carrier densities where exotic correlated states emerge. We introduce optical voltage profiling, a noninvasive wide-field technique that replaces local voltage probes with a proximal monolayer MoSe$_2$ exciton sensor. Isolated by thin hexagonal boron nitride, this sensor converts the target's local electrostatic potential into spatially resolved modulations of exciton reflectance. Through pixel-wise in situ calibration, these signals yield quantitative two-dimensional voltage maps of an actively biased semiconductor device. Using this method, we demonstrate the carrier-density-driven metal-insulator transition in bilayer MoSe$_2$ and obtain channel resistances below 1 k$Ω$ despite M$Ω$-scale two-terminal resistances in the metallic region. The optically derived resistance exhibits a metal-insulator crossover near the resistance quantum $h/e^2$, and the voltage maps and reconstructed local conductivity reveal pronounced spatial heterogeneity in both insulating and metallic regimes. Beyond resolving channel resistance under high contact-resistance conditions, the technique provides spatially resolved access to microscopic transport heterogeneity in functional van der Waals devices.

</details>


### [130] [Isolating the natural edges of bilayer graphene in gate-defined mesoscopic devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18384)
*Francesco Blanda,Grazia Raciti,Thilo Glatzel,Aurin Strathmann,Fabrizio Volante,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Ilaria Zardo,Thomas Ihn,Klaus Ensslin,Andrea Hofmann*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种石墨-栅极（graphite-gated）架构的双层石墨烯（bilayer graphene）器件，通过单层图案化石墨栅极实现完全静电定义的霍尔条（Hall-bar）。测量揭示了介观输运（mesoscopic transport）特征，包括霍尔效应淬灭（Hall-effect quenching）和磁阻峰（magnetoresistance peaks），这些特征归因于边界散射（boundary scattering）效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统石墨烯器件中，活性区域往往受到天然石墨边缘的影响，导致边界无序和不可控的输运特性。作者希望通过将器件与自然边缘完全隔离，实现更可控的静电定义器件结构，并研究边界散射对输运性质的影响。

Method: 采用单一图案化石墨栅极层，构建石墨烯双栅极器件架构，使活性区域完全隔离于天然石墨边缘，实现全静电定义的霍尔条形器件。通过纵向和霍尔测量（Hall measurements）表征输运性质，并利用拉曼光谱（Raman spectroscopy）和开尔文探针力显微镜（Kelvin probe force microscopy）表征边界无序程度。同时以相同架构制备场效应晶体管（field-effect transistor）进行对比测试。

Result: 介观输运特征随载流子密度的变化表明有效沟道宽度随费米能级（Fermi level）的升高而增大，载流子在静电边界处发生散射；谱学和显微表征揭示边界因制备工艺而存在无序。对比实验中，同架构场效应晶体管的量子迁移率（quantum mobility）不受边界散射限制，且在低至4 mT磁场的条件下仍可见量子振荡，对应迁移率达2.5 × 10^6 cm²/Vs。

Conclusion: 石墨栅极架构能够实现完全静电定义的器件边界，使载流子散射主要发生在静电定义边界处；同架构场效应晶体管可实现无边界散射限制的高量子迁移率，表明该架构在同时实现可控边界和优异电学性能方面具有潜力。

Abstract: We introduce a graphite-gated architecture for bilayer graphene devices in which the active device is completely isolated from the natural graphene edges. Using a single patterned graphite-gate layer, we realize a fully electrostatically defined Hall-bar. Longitudinal and Hall measurements reveal mesoscopic transport features, including Hall-effect quenching and magnetoresistance peaks associated with boundary scattering. The dependence of the mesoscopic features on the carrierdensity shows that the effective channel width increases with the Fermi level and the electrostatic confinement at the gate-defined boundaries, and indicates that the carriers scatter at the electrostatic boundary. Raman spectroscopy and Kelvin probe force microscopy suggest that this boundary is disordered due to the used fabrication methods. Comparably, the quantum mobility in a fieldeffect transistor fabricated with the same architecture is not limited by boundary scattering and the visibility of quantum oscillations down to 4 mT suggests a record value of 2.5 x 10^6 cm2/Vs.

</details>


### [131] [Superfast hole spin qubits enabled by uniaxial strain-boosted spin-orbit coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18483)
*Yi-Xu Wang,Yang Liu,Shan Guan,Jun-Wei Luo,Shu-Shen Li*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该论文表明，利用互补金属氧化物半导体（CMOS）技术可及的应变可显著增强Ge/SiGe量子阱顶层空穴子带的线性Rashba自旋轨道耦合强度，使其可与二维Rashba材料相媲美，从而将空穴自旋量子比特的Rabi频率提升两个数量级至前所未有的40 GHz，并进入对栅控制引入的电场噪声免疫的新区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维电子/空穴气体在半导体异质结构中受限于较弱的Rashba自旋轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling, SOC），难以通过电场而非磁场来有效操控自旋自由度，因此需要探索增强SOC的有效途径。

Method: 通过利用CMOS技术可及的应变手段，增强Ge/SiGe量子阱中顶层空穴子带的轻空穴（light-hole）与重空穴（heavy-hole）能带混合，从而大幅提升线性Rashba SOC强度。

Result: 增强后的Rashba SOC将Ge/SiGe量子阱中空穴自旋量子比特的Rabi频率提升两个数量级至40 GHz，比其他量子比特平台快一个数量级以上；且当Rabi频率超过25 GHz时，量子比特进入对栅控电场噪声免疫的新工作区间。

Conclusion: 该研究为在二维半导体空穴气体中大幅增强Rashba SOC提供了新途径，使其达到适合自旋电子学应用的水平，同时可同时提升栅控速度和栅控保真度。

Abstract: Two-dimensional (2D) electron/hole gases confined in semiconductor heterostructures suffer from weak Rashba spin-orbit coupling (SOC) for manipulating spin degreee of freedom via an electric rather than a magnetic field. Here, we show that complementary metal-oxide-semiconductor technology-accessible strain could substantially enhance the linear Rashba SOC of the top hole subband in Ge/SiGe quantum wells (QWs) to a level comparable to that of 2D Rashba materials through enhancing the mixture of the light-hole and heavy-hole bands. We further show that strongly enhanced Rashba SOC boosts the Rabi frequency of hole spin qubits confined in Ge/SiGe QWs by two orders of magnitude to an unprecedented 40 GHz, more than one order of magnitude faster than other qubit platforms. We also demonstrate that the hole spin rotation with Rabi frequency > 25 GHz enters a new regime being immune to gate control-induced electric noise, opening a new avenue to simultaneously improve the gate speed and gate fidelity. Our findings provide a new routine to substantially enhance the Rashba SOC in 2D semiconductor hole gases to a level that is great for spintronic applications.

</details>


### [132] [Floquet engineering of topological bands in semiconductor van der Waals heterobilayers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18556)
*Eréndira Santana-Suárez,Brayan E. Walteros-Mendivelso,A. Jazmín Tapia-de-la-Rosa,Mahmoud M. Asmar,David A. Ruiz-Tijerina*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文证明近红外至可见光的周期性驱动可在过渡金属二硫化物异质双层的光子修饰能带中诱导拓扑相变，通过Floquet形式论获得有效四带模型，实现了陈数高达±2的拓扑相。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在固态介质中通过光驱动实现拓扑相变的可能性，特别是寻找能产生更高陈数（higher Chern numbers）拓扑能带的候选材料平台。

Method: 将Floquet形式论（Floquet formalism）应用于光耦合的最低阶$\mathbf{k}\cdot\mathbf{p}$哈密顿量，构建有效四带模型，用以捕捉II型TMD异质双层在第一光子共振附近的本质光子修饰能带。

Result: 零光子与单光子扇区之间的交叉有效反转能带，产生陈数最高达±2的拓扑相，能隙量级约为10 meV。

Conclusion: 研究结果确立了TMD材料作为工程化高陈数能带的主要候选体系，并为在固态介质中探索驱动拓扑态提供了新途径。

Abstract: We show that periodic driving with near-infrared to visible light drives topological phase transitions in the photon-dressed band structure of transition-metal dichalcogenide heterobilayers. We apply the Floquet formalism to a light-coupled lowest-order $\mathbf{k}\cdot\mathbf{p}$ Hamiltonian, and obtain an effective four-band model that correctly captures the essential photon-dressed bands of type-II TMD heterobilayers in the vicinity of the first photon resonance. Crossings between the zero- and one-photon sectors effectively invert the bands, yielding topological phases with Chern numbers up to $\pm 2$ and gaps of order 10 meV. Our results establish TMDs as prime candidates for engineering bands with higher Chern numbers, and exploring driven topological states in solid state media.

</details>


### [133] [A miniature evaporator for in-operando deposition of isolated atoms in a low-temperature scanning tunneling microscope](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18741)
*Jeongmin Oh,Hermann Osterhage,Vasily Cherepanov,Sven Just,Denis Krylov,F. Stefan Tautz,Taner Esat,Ruslan Temirov*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文报告了一种集成在毫开尔文（millikelvin）扫描隧道显微镜（scanning tunneling microscope, STM）头部的紧凑型毫瓦级蒸发源，由商用微型白炽灯改造而成，可在近5 K低温下向MgO/Ag(100)表面沉积孤立Fe原子，且沉积前后可保持对同一纳米级表面区域的成像访问。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在低温STM中对冷样品进行稀释原子系综沉积长期存在挑战，因为辐射屏蔽和受限的内部几何结构通常阻碍直接的沉积路径，尤其在为毫开尔文运行设计的仪器中更为突出。

Method: 采用商用微型白炽灯制造的紧凑型毫瓦级蒸发源，将裸露的钨丝涂覆微米级Fe膜，并直接集成到毫开尔文STM头部，距离样品约1 cm处进行蒸发沉积。

Result: 在约5 K下成功将孤立Fe原子沉积到MgO/Ag(100)表面，蒸发仅使STM本体温度升高约2 K，沉积后同一纳米级表面区域横向位移小于5 nm即可扫描；微分电导谱在±14 mV附近呈现对称的非弹性台阶，确认沉积原子为Fe；估算局部沉积通量为1.5×10⁻⁵ nm⁻²·s⁻¹，对应标称蒸发器寿命约150 h。

Conclusion: 固定式蒸发器可在无需室温视线、可移动辐射屏蔽或冷却后机械访问蒸发器的条件下实现重复低通量沉积，同时保持沉积前后对同一原子尺度表面区域的访问能力。

Abstract: Depositing dilute atomic ensembles onto cold samples is challenging in low-temperature scanning tunneling microscopes (STM) because radiation shields and restricted internal geometries often preclude a direct deposition path, particularly in instruments designed for millikelvin operation. We present a compact, milliwatt-range evaporation source fabricated from a commercial miniature incandescent lamp and integrated directly into a millikelvin STM head. The exposed tungsten filament is coated with a micrometre-thick Fe film and positioned about 1 cm from the sample. We deposit isolated Fe atoms onto MgO/Ag(100) while operating the microscope near 5 K. Evaporation increases the STM-body temperature by only about 2 K, and the same nanoscopic surface region can be readily scanned after deposition with a lateral displacement of less than 5 nm. Differential-conductance spectra displaying symmetric inelastic steps near $\pm$14 mV identify the deposited atoms on MgO as Fe. From STM images, we estimate a local deposition flux of $1.5\times10^{-5}~\mathrm{nm^{-2}\,s^{-1}}$, corresponding to a nominal evaporator lifetime of ~150 h. The fixed evaporator enables repeated low-flux deposition without a room-temperature line of sight, the need for movable radiation shields, or mechanical evaporator access after cooldown, while preserving access to the same atomic-scale surface region before and after deposition.

</details>


### [134] [Electromagnetic and Acoustic Fano Interference in Surface Acoustic Wave Resonators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18771)
*P. K. Rath,J. M. Kitzman,M. Mesbah,C. Undershute,J. Pollanen*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文在GHz频段表面声波（surface acoustic wave, SAW）谐振器中同时引入电磁与声学两路径Fano干涉（Fano interference），通过系统调节谐振器的声学反射率实现两种干涉机制的独立分离与分析，并展示了从室温到低温的宽温度工作范围，为经典与量子传感应用提供了可能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表面声波谐振器是凝聚态系统的灵敏探针及表面选择性传感器，并已集成至与量子比特（qubit）平台混合的量子系统中用于量子信息处理与传感。现有方法通过优化器件架构和电学测量技术提升灵敏度，而利用干涉效应雕琢谱对称性被视为进一步提升灵敏度的另一条有前景的途径。

Method: 在GHz频段表面声波谐振器中同时引入电磁与声学两类Fano干涉以塑造其光谱响应；通过系统地改变谐振器的声学反射率，实现每种干涉机制的独立隔离与分析。

Result: 成功同时实现电磁与声学Fano干涉对GHz表面声波谐振器光谱响应的调控，且可通过调节声学反射率分别考察两类干涉机制。器件在从环境温度到低温的宽温度范围内均可工作。

Conclusion: 同时引入电磁与声学Fano干涉为增强表面声波谐振器灵敏度提供了新路径，其宽温度工作范围表明该方法在经典与量子传感应用中均具有潜力。

Abstract: Surface acoustic wave-based resonators are sensitive probes of condensed matter systems, as well as surface-selective sensors for chemistry and biology. Surface acoustic wave devices have also been integrated into hybrid quantum systems with qubit platforms for applications in quantum information processing and sensing. The sensitivity of piezoelectric surface wave-based resonators to investigate these various systems can be enhanced by optimizing the device architecture and electrical measurement techniques. Alternatively, tailoring the spectral symmetry, arising from interference effects, offers a promising route to further improve sensitivity. In this work, we demonstrate the simultaneous introduction of both electromagnetic and acoustic Fano interference to shape the spectral response of GHz-frequency surface acoustic wave resonators. By systematically modifying the acoustic reflectivity of the resonators, we are able to isolate and analyze each interference mechanism independently. The broad range of temperature operation, from ambient to cryogenic temperatures highlights the potential for both classical and quantum sensing applications.

</details>


### [135] [Spectral Fingerprints of Resonant Defect Scattering by Substitutional Mn in Graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18885)
*Ahmed Samir Lotfy,Zviadi Zarkua,Renan Villarreal,Rikkie Joris,Muhammad Saad,Koen van Stiphout,Karina Landivar,Steven Brems,Giovanni Di Santo,Aleksandr Seliverstov,Simona Achilli,Luca Petaccia,Lino M. C. Pereira*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本研究以石墨烯/Cu(111)上的替代锰（substitutional Mn）原子为模型点缺陷，结合扫描隧道显微镜（STM）与角分辨光电子谱（ARPES），验证了共振散射（resonant scattering）的预测指纹特征。当锰浓度增加至0.44%时，狄拉克点（Dirac point）拉伸（stretching）达到0.52 eV，动量展宽（momentum broadening）则保持能量无关。这些光谱指纹将替代锰归类为强共振散射体，并确立了STM与ARPES联用作为表征石墨烯中共振无序（resonant disorder）的有效方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在石墨烯中，共振散射是一种重要的量子输运机制，其实验指纹此前缺乏系统的验证。作者选择替代锰作为模型点缺陷，旨在通过实验手段检验理论预测的共振散射特征，并探索一种可靠的表征方案。

Method: 以石墨烯/Cu(111)上的替代锰为模型点缺陷，将扫描隧道显微镜与角分辨光电子谱两种实验手段相结合，系统测量锰浓度变化下的电子结构光谱特征，包括狄拉克点拉伸与动量展宽。

Result: 随着锰浓度升高至0.44%，狄拉克点拉伸达到0.52 eV；动量展宽在整个能量范围内保持恒定（能量无关），这两项光谱指纹共同表明替代锰属于强共振散射体（strong resonant scatterer）。

Conclusion: 实验确认了替代锰在石墨烯中表现为强共振散射体，验证了共振散射的理论预测；同时证明STM与ARPES的联用是识别和表征石墨烯中共振无序的有效手段。

Abstract: Using substitutional Mn in graphene/Cu(111) as a model point defect, we combine scanning tunneling microscopy (STM) and angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy (ARPES) to test the predicted fingerprints of resonant scattering. As the Mn concentration increases to 0.44%, the Dirac point stretching reaches 0.52 eV, while momentum broadening remains energy independent. These spectral fingerprints classify substitutional Mn as a strong resonant scatterer and establish the combination of STM and ARPES as a powerful approach to identify and characterize resonant disorder in graphene.

</details>


### [136] [Flat and Topological Floquet Minibands from Patterned Light in Untwisted Bilayer Graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18945)
*Muhammad Faisal,Micheal Vogl*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种替代物理扭转或应变的方法：利用图案化的电磁场光学印刻超晶格，在AA和AB堆叠的双层石墨烯中产生平带和拓扑相。该方法中超晶格周期由光照决定，易于调节，且圆偏振光破坏时间反演对称性，计算谷陈数发现丰富的拓扑结构和多次相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 范德华材料中的二维超晶格可承载平带和非平凡拓扑，最著名的是扭转双层石墨烯中的魔角。然而这类超晶格通常通过扭转或应变实现，样品制备后周期固定且极难调节，限制了其作为可调平台的研究与应用。

Method: 采用图案化面内圆偏振光，以及由图案化面外纵向场与均匀圆偏振场组成的联合驱动，在AA和AB堆叠的双层石墨烯中光学印刻超晶格。通过计算中央能带的谷陈数（valley Chern number）来表征其拓扑结构，并考察堆叠和驱动条件对能带结构与拓扑性质的影响。

Result: 两种驱动方案在AA和AB堆叠的双层石墨烯中均产生隔离能带；AB堆叠中中央能带趋于近平带，捕获了驱动莫尔（moiré）超晶格的关键特征。计算谷陈数发现两种堆叠下均呈现丰富的拓扑结构，包含多次拓扑相变。

Conclusion: 光照诱导超晶格被确立为一种灵活可调的平台，可用于在双层石墨烯中工程化平带和拓扑相。该方法超晶格周期由照明设定、易于调节，且圆偏振光破坏时间反演对称性，为研究平带物理及相关拓扑相提供了新的实验途径。

Abstract: Two-dimensional superlattices in van der Waals materials host flat bands and nontrivial topology, most famously at the so-called magic angles of twisted bilayer graphene, where flat bands give rise to correlated and topological phases. Yet these superlattices are usually created by twisting the layers or applying strain, and once a sample is fabricated, their period is fixed and extremely difficult to tune. Here we propose an alternative route: imprint the superlattice optically, using patterned electromagnetic fields rather than a physical twist or strain. We show that patterned in-plane cir- cularly polarized light and a combined drive consisting of a patterned out-of-plane longitudinal field and a uniform circularly polarized field produce isolated bands in both AA- and AB-stacked bilayer graphene. In AB stacking, the central bands additionally become nearly flat, capturing key features of a driven moir/'e superlattice. In this approach, the superlattice period is set by the illumination and is straightforward to tune, and circularly polarized light breaks time-reversal symmetry. Computing the valley Chern numbers of the central bands, we find a rich topological structure with several phase transitions in both stackings. Our results establish light-induced superlattices as a flexible and tunable platform for engineering flat bands and topological phases in bilayer graphene.

</details>


### [137] [Ultra-low energy (20 - 300 eV) electron imaging and diffraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19113)
*Tatiana Latychevskaia*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文是一篇讲座性文献，系统讨论了低能电子（low-energy electron）相较于商用透射电子显微镜（transmission electron microscope, TEM）中使用的常规能量电子所表现出的性质差异，并介绍了低能电子衍射（low-energy electron diffraction）和低能电子全息（low-energy electron holography）的原理与实验方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低能电子在材料表面结构表征中具有独特优势，但相较于商用透射电子显微镜中的常规能量电子，其性质尚需系统性阐述；该讲座旨在对比两类电子的性质差异，以阐明低能电子的适用范围与价值。

Method: 采用讲座形式，首先对比低能电子与常规能量电子的性质差异，随后分别阐述低能电子衍射和低能电子全息的原理，并给出相应的实验方案（experimental scheme）。

Result: 摘要未提供具体数值或定量结果，仅呈现了低能电子衍射与低能电子全息两种技术的基本原理框架和实验方案展示。

Conclusion: 低能电子在特定表征场景中具有区别于常规能量电子的性质，低能电子衍射与低能电子全息可作为有力的表面结构研究手段。

Abstract: The lecture discusses the properties of low-energy electrons compared to the electrons of conventional energies employed in commercial transmission electron microscopes. Low-energy electron diffraction and low-energy electron holography principles and experimental schemes are presented and discussed.

</details>

### [138] [Chern insulator boundary criticality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18199)
*Benjamin Moy,Eduardo Fradkin*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究了存在边界时陈绝缘体相变（Chern insulator transition）处的手征性（chirality）特征。作者利用狄拉克质量畴壁构造，证明临界点上手征边缘模离域到体相中，但边界费米子关联函数仍保留手征结构并获得体相费米子的标度维度。通过计算电流关联函数，作者展示了体相霍尔响应的反常与边界附近离域手征模之间的匹配关系，并仅利用半空间（2+1）维共形场论（conformal field theory, CFT）的剩余共形对称性，识别出电流和能动张量关联函数中的奇宇称项，这些项编码了有关模式并决定了其电磁和引力反常系数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 陈绝缘体相变在无边界时已由无质量狄拉克费米子描述，其宇称反常给出临界霍尔电导σ_xy=e²/2h。然而，真实物理系统存在边界，边界如何影响临界行为、手征边缘模在临界点的命运以及体相反常如何与边界模匹配，尚未被系统解析。

Method: 作者采用狄拉克质量畴壁构造来研究平庸绝缘体与陈数为1（C=1）的陈绝缘体之间的相变。利用半空间中（2+1）维CFT的剩余共形对称性，计算电流和能动张量关联函数，并识别其中编码离域手征模的奇宇称项。

Result: 证明临界点上手征边缘模离域到体相中，但边界费米子关联函数仍保持手征结构，其标度维度与体相费米子相同。体相霍尔响应的反常由边界附近的离域手征模匹配。获得的电磁和引力反常系数可推广到一般破缺时间反演对称性（time-reversal breaking）的（2+1）维CFT，超出自由狄拉克相变范畴。

Conclusion: 分析框架适用于广义的时间反演破缺（2+1）维CFT，不局限于自由狄拉克相变。研究还扩展到更高陈数（higher Chern number）相变，以及（3+1）维拓扑绝缘体与平庸绝缘体之间的相变。

Abstract: We investigate signatures of chirality at Chern insulator transitions in the presence of a boundary. The transition between a trivial insulator and a Chern insulator with Chern number $C=1$ is described by a massless Dirac fermion whose parity anomaly gives a critical Hall conductivity $σ_{xy}=\frac{1}{2}\frac{e^2}{h}$. Using a Dirac mass domain wall construction, we show that the chiral edge mode delocalizes into the bulk at criticality, but the boundary fermion correlation function retains a chiral structure and acquires the scaling dimension of the bulk fermion. We compute current correlation functions and demonstrate that the anomaly of the bulk Hall response is matched by delocalized chiral modes near the boundary. Using only the residual conformal symmetry of a (2+1)d conformal field theory (CFT) in a half-space, we identify parity-odd terms in current and energy-momentum tensor correlation functions that encode these modes and determine their electromagnetic and gravitational anomaly coefficients. Our analysis therefore applies to general time-reversal breaking (2+1)d CFTs, beyond the free Dirac transition. We also extend our results to higher Chern number transitions and to the transition between a (3+1)d topological insulator and a trivial insulator.

</details>


### [139] [Finite-temperature Green's function cluster expansion from thermofield doubles: Breakdown of the polaron picture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18267)
*M. R. Carbone,S. Fomichev,B. Kloss,A. J. Millis,M. Berciu,D. R. Reichman,J. Sous*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出一种原则上数值精确的有限温度绿函数簇展开方法（finite-temperature Green's function cluster expansion），用于计算极化子（polaron）在有限温度下动量与频率分辨的单粒子绿函数（single-particle Green's function）。该方法将广义绿函数簇展开与热场双形式（thermofield double formalism）结合，并在一维Holstein极化子上进行基准测试，在零时间演化或无解析延拓的条件下直接于频率空间恢复动量分辨谱，与有限温度密度矩阵重整化群（DMRG）结果定量吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有动量平均族方法（momentum average family）仅能精确求解零温度下的极化子问题，而有限温度下如何高效且数值精确地计算极化子的光谱性质仍缺乏直接方案。传统方法通常需要时间演化或解析延拓（analytic continuation），在有限温度和中等耦合区间面临数值难题。

Method: 方法由两部分构成：一是广义绿函数簇展开（generalized Green's function cluster expansion），通过受限声子云（phonon cloud）构型的运动方程层级零温度求解极化子问题；二是热场双形式，将热迹（thermal trace）映射到倍增希尔伯特空间（doubled Hilbert space）上的纯态期望值。两者结合后，运动方程与多玻色子零温度理论具有相同代数结构，温度通过类Bogoliubov混合角（Bogoliubov-type mixing angle）进入，控制与一组虚构浴玻色子（fictitious bath bosons）的耦合。

Result: 在一维Holstein极化子上实现了该方法，计算了谱函数、色散关系、寿命和有效质量，覆盖不同耦合强度区间及温度至T/Ω~1。与可用的有限温度DMRG结果相比，在可负担的计算成本下取得定量一致；谱直接在频率空间获得，无需时间演化或解析延拓。作者同时指出倍增声子希尔伯特空间具有实与虚构云的竞争性非平凡构型结构，云参数的收敛需谨慎处理。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了有限温度下极化子光谱计算的一条数值精确且计算成本可接受的途径，弥补了动量平均类方法局限于零温的空白。其直接频率空间求解的机制避免了传统方法的时间演化与解析延拓步骤，并在开源软件包中实现供社区使用。后续需关注倍增声子空间中云参数收敛性的进一步优化。

Abstract: We introduce a method, numerically exact in principle, for computing the momentum- and frequency-resolved single-particle Green's function of a polaron at finite temperature. The method, which we refer to as the finite-temperature Green's function cluster expansion, combines two ingredients: the generalized Green's function cluster expansion, a numerically exact extension of the momentum average family of methods that solves the polaron problem at zero temperature through a hierarchy of equations of motion for restricted phonon cloud configurations; and the thermofield double formalism, which maps the thermal trace onto a pure-state expectation value over a doubled Hilbert space. The resulting equations of motion have the same algebraic structure as those of the multi-boson zero-temperature theory, with the temperature entering through a Bogoliubov-type mixing angle that controls the coupling to a set of fictitious bath bosons. We implement the method in our open-source software package and benchmark it on the one-dimensional Holstein polaron, computing spectral functions, dispersions, lifetimes, and effective masses across coupling regimes and temperatures up to $T/Ω\sim 1$. Where finite-temperature density matrix renormalization group results are available, we find quantitative agreement at affordable computational cost. The method recovers momentum-resolved spectra directly in frequency space, with no time evolution or analytic continuation. We also discuss the practical costs of the approach. In particular, since the doubled phonon Hilbert space has a non-trivial configuration structure in which real and fictitious clouds compete, convergence in the corresponding cloud parameters requires care.

</details>


### [140] [Rare-earth spin textures and a route to electronic inhomogeneity in $Pr_2Ir_2O_7$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18301)
*Kyle G Sherman,Michael J Lawler*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过将蒙特卡洛采样的Pr自旋冰构型与Ir电子的八能带Hartree-Fock模型耦合，研究表明在烧绿石铱氧化物Pr₂Ir₂O₇中，受挫的镨自旋纹理（frustrated praseodymium spin texture）可引发费米面附近态密度的空间调制，进而通过Doniach灵敏度机制倾斜局部Kondo屏蔽与磁有序之间的平衡，为实验中观测到的Kondo屏蔽/破坏不均匀性提供了理由驱动的路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Pr₂Ir₂O₇在低温下保持金属性，与其他同类物质绝缘不同；近期扫描隧道谱实验发现其表面在不同解理温度下呈现Kondo屏蔽区域与Kondo破坏区域的不均匀混合或均匀分布。作者试图回答受挫的镨自旋纹理是否为这种不均匀性的种子来源。

Method: 生成蒙特卡洛（Monte-Carlo）采样的Pr自旋冰构型，假定其随时间缓慢演化，并通过局域Kondo交换耦合J_K将其与Ir电子的八能带Hartree-Fock模型耦合；同时在小簇上扫描大的Pr表面以生成合成隧道图谱（tunneling map）。

Result: 研究发现成员族中常见的全入全出（all-in-all-out）态的电荷能隙随耦合J_K平滑闭合；新提出的富含磁单极的“水母”（jellyfish）纹理进一步抑制绝缘行为约ΔJ_Kc≈0.03t；合成隧道图谱在表面分裂为岛屿状结构。经典可模拟理论虽然省略了Kondo单重态，但其产生的费米面态密度空间调制足以通过Doniach指数的Kondo温度灵敏度倾斜局部分子屏蔽与磁序之间的平衡。

Conclusion: 受挫的自旋纹理可通过经典可模拟的途径触发Kondo/Kondo破坏的不均匀性，为实验观测到的Pr₂Ir₂O₇表面不均匀现象提供了理由驱动（frustration-driven）的解释机制。

Abstract: The pyrochlore iridate $Pr_2Ir_2O_7$ remains metallic at low temperatures where its family members insulate. Recently, scanning tunneling spectroscopy experiments have revealed an inhomogeneous mixture of Kondo-screened and Kondo-destroyed regions when its surface is produced by cleaving at room temperature while it remains uniform when cleaved at low temperatures. We ask whether the frustrated praseodymium spin texture seeds this inhomogeneity. To study this, we produce Monte-Carlo-sampled Pr spin-ice configurations, presumed to evolve slowly in time, and couple through a local Kondo exchange $J_K$ to an eight-band Hartree-Fock model of the Ir electrons.
  We further find the charge gap of the all-in-all-out state common in the other family members closes smoothly with the coupling $J_K$, and the recently proposed monopole-rich ``jellyfish'' textures further suppress the insulating behavior by $ΔJ_{Kc}\approx 0.03\,t$. Additionally, scanning a small cluster across a large Pr surface yields a synthetic tunneling map that fractures into islands within a sea. Although our classical simulable theory omits the Kondo singlet, the spatial modulation of the Fermi-level density of states it produces is, through the exponential Doniach sensitivity of the Kondo temperature, sufficient to tip the local balance between screening and magnetic order; a frustration-driven route to the observed Kondo/Kondo-destroyed inhomogeneity.

</details>


### [141] [Topology is not silent in the transport noise of multiband bosons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18527)
*Zhi-Wei Wang,Samuel L. Braunstein*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文指出，费米面上的电子由于只占据表面态，其Berry曲率的拓扑部分不会在输运噪声中显现——反常霍尔电导是量子化的，但霍尔电流的涨落不携带陈数（Chern number）信息。对于玻色子系统（如磁子、声子），热布居使得每个能带都被占据且陈数各异，因此曲率的调和部分不再是常数，涨落可以揭示带几何信息。作者从含涨落的玻尔兹曼方程（Boltzmann equation）推导出一个无零温类比的新物理量，即按占据权重加权的陈数分散度，并证明在平衡态下通过反称电流互谱（cross-spectrum）的频率分辨求和规则可观测该量，具体协议适用于Cu(1,3-bdc)材料。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 费米面上电子的Berry曲率拓扑部分在输运噪声中完全消失，反常霍尔电导量子化但涨落不含陈数信息。作者质疑这一抵消是否为费米面的偶然性质，转而考察玻色子系统，因为磁子和声子热布居每个能带，每个能带各有自己的陈数，导致曲率的调和分解不再是单一常数。

Method: 本文从含涨落的玻尔兹曼方程（fluctuating Boltzmann equation）出发进行推导，建立电流噪声求和规则。作者将能量-磁化相减项定义为有界磁化密度（bounded magnetization density）的旋度，从而证明其在每次实现（realization by realization）中对输运测量贡献严格为零。文中对驱动协议与平衡协议进行比较，并推导出了反称电流互谱的频率分辨求和规则。

Result: 作者发现新的物理量——按能带占据权重加权的陈数分散度（occupation-weighted dispersion of Chern numbers）——仅当多个能带被不同权重的热布居占据时存在，没有零温类比。驱动协议不可行（噪声低于平衡基底十二个数量级），但平衡电流互谱的反称部分的频率分辨求和规则可分离拓扑与几何内容；聚频谱（frequency-integrated form）则无法做到。作者将协议具体化到Cu(1,3-bdc)材料，并验证了对模型完备性、色散关系、测温与磁场标定的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 玻色子系统的热布居使带几何（band geometry）在输运噪声中可见，这是费米子系统所不具备的新效应。该效应可从平衡态电流互谱的频率分辨求和规则中提取，并已在具体候选材料Cu(1,3-bdc)上给出可实施且鲁棒的测量协议。

Abstract: For electrons at a Fermi surface, the topological part of the Berry curvature drops out of the transport noise: the anomalous Hall conductivity is quantized, and the fluctuations of the Hall current know nothing about the Chern number. We show that this cancellation is an accident of the Fermi surface, and that it fails for bosons. Magnons and phonons thermally populate every band, each with its own Chern number, so the harmonic sector of the Hodge decomposition of the curvature is not a single constant and a variance does not annihilate it. What survives is the occupation-weighted dispersion of Chern numbers across populated bands. That band geometry is visible in current noise is known, and at zero temperature the antisymmetric part of the noise sum rule already returns a Chern number; what is new is an object with no zero-temperature analogue, existing only when several bands are populated and weighted differently. We derive it from a fluctuating Boltzmann equation and settle the energy-magnetization subtraction at the level of fluctuations: the subtracted term is the curl of a bounded magnetization density, so it contributes exactly zero to the current any transport measurement records, realization by realization. Is the quantity measurable? A driven protocol is not: the excess noise sits twelve orders below the equilibrium floor. An equilibrium protocol is, through a sum rule on the antisymmetric part of the current cross-spectrum, which carries no drive and hence no suppression. We prove that the frequency-integrated form of that sum rule cannot separate the topological from the geometric content, and show that the frequency-resolved cross-spectrum does separate them at accessible resolution. The protocol is specified for Cu(1,3-bdc), with its robustness to nuisance-model completeness and to dispersion, thermometry and field calibration.

</details>


### [142] [Universal thermal breakdown of polaron coherence in one, two, and three dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18529)
*Jeet Shannigrahi,Janez Bonča,Mona Berciu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文在二维方晶格和三维简单立方晶格上求解了玻拉龙（polaron）在有限温度下失去准粒子相干性的问题，发现有效质量和逆寿命随温度单调增长，直到温度约为声子频率量级（T ~ 0.5Ω）时准粒子峰溶解为宽的非相干热连续谱。研究提出了动量平均（Momentum Average, MA）近似的有限温度推广，得到闭式自能表达式，在强耦合极限下对所有温度渐近精确，并通过一维数值精确方法进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 玻拉龙在升高的温度下如何失去其准粒子相干性是一个长期未解的问题。Holstein于1959年仅在最极端条件（反绝热极限、强耦合）下处理了该问题，而近期的数值精确方法大多局限于一维，缺乏对更高维度及更广泛耦合区的系统理解。

Method: 本研究提出了动量平均（Momentum Average, MA）近似的有限温度推广，得到基于费曼图推导的闭式自能表达式，该自能形式在强耦合极限下对所有温度渐近精确。研究在二维方晶格及三维简单立方晶格上覆盖弱耦合、中等耦合和强耦合三个区域进行系统求解，并与数值精确的一维方法（变分精确对角化-有限温度Lanczos方法，VED-FTLM，以及有限温度密度矩阵重整化群，finite-T DMRG）进行基准对比。

Result: 研究发现玻拉龙有效质量m*和逆寿命1/τ随温度单调增加，直至温度T ~ 0.5Ω时准粒子峰溶解为宽的非相干热连续谱。声子频率Ω因此构成一个普适的相干性尺度，与体系维度和耦合强度无关，验证了Holstein的预测远超其推导所限的范围。一维谱函数的基准对比表明，该方法与数值精确的VED-FTLM和有限温度DMRG结果具有极好的定量一致性。

Conclusion: 声子频率Ω确立了玻拉龙准粒子相干性的普适温度标度，这一结论在弱、中等和强耦合区间以及二维和三维格点体系中都成立。所提出的有限温度动量平均近似为研究有限温度玻拉龙性质提供了一种既具解析封闭性又高度精确的理论工具。

Abstract: How a polaron loses its quasiparticle coherence with increasing temperature is a long-standing open problem. Holstein addressed it in 1959 only in the extreme antiadiabatic, strong-coupling limit, while more recent numerically exact approaches are largely restricted to one dimension. Here we solve this problem on square and simple cubic lattices across the weak-, intermediate-, and strong-coupling regimes. We show that the polaron effective mass $m^*$ and inverse lifetime $1/τ$ increase monotonically with temperature until, at $T \sim 0.5\,Ω$, the quasiparticle peak dissolves into a broad incoherent thermal continuum. The phonon frequency $Ω$ therefore defines a universal coherence scale, independent of dimensionality and coupling strength, validating Holstein's prediction far beyond the regime in which it was derived. These results follow from a finite-temperature generalization of the Momentum Average (MA) approximation, yielding a closed-form, diagrammatically derived self-energy that is asymptotically exact in the strong-coupling limit at all temperatures. Benchmark comparisons demonstrate excellent quantitative agreement of the resulting 1D spectral functions with the numerically exact Variational Exact Diagonalization-Finite-Temperature Lanczos Method (VED-FTLM) and finite-$T$ Density Matrix Renormalization Group (DMRG).

</details>


### [143] [High-field fate of the Kitaev quantum spin liquid in $α$-RuCl$_3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18530)
*K. Imamura,R. Ohno,Y. C. Tsuzuki,Y. Akui,R. Namba,K. Ishihara,M. Akaki,M. Kimata,N. Kurita,H. Tanaka,N. Kimura,S. Imajo,A. Matsuo,K. Kindo,Y. Matsuda,K. Hashimoto,Y. Mizukami,T. Shibauchi*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过高达24 T的高场比热（specific heat）测量，报道了α-RuCl₃中Kitaev量子自旋液体（Kitaev quantum spin liquids, KQSL）在约15 T处经历一个明显交叉（crossover），但即使在约90%磁化饱和下，KQSL的特征——非平庸的马约拉纳（Majorana）与涡旋（vison）激发签名及其面内磁化各向异性——仍然保留，表明该相持续存在于非微扰区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kitaev量子自旋液体承载分数化激发（itinerant Majorana准粒子与有能隙的Z₂涡旋），但强磁场下其是否存活仍属悬而未决的问题。α-RuCl₃作为领先候选材料，其从量子无序相到自旋极化态之间的微观演化尚未被阐明，亟需高场热力学测量揭示这一过渡的微观图景。

Method: 作者对α-RuCl₃单晶进行了高达24 T的高场比热测量，并结合面内磁场角度旋转以探测比热的角向调制（six-fold angular modulation），同时分析了激发能隙对磁场的依赖关系及其与H³标度律的偏离，并与磁化数据的各向异性行为进行对比。

Result: 测量揭示了在μ₀H*≈15 T处存在一个明确的交叉：超过该场强，比热的六重角向调制消失，激发能隙偏离H³标度。然而，能隙随场增大而减小，且面内磁化各向异性持续存在至约24 T，这与平凡的自旋极化态形成鲜明对比，表明KQSL签名即使在约90%磁化饱和下仍然保留。

Conclusion: KQSL在α-RuCl₃中不仅存在于微扰窗口内，还延伸至非微扰区域，其中Majorana与vison的能标逐渐合并，最终才让位于自旋极化态。这项热力学路线图为理解分数化相在强磁场下的演化提供了基础。

Abstract: Kitaev quantum spin liquids (KQSLs) host fractionalized excitations described by itinerant Majorana quasiparticles and gapped $Z_2$ fluxes (visons), providing a platform for emergent topological matter. Whether such a state survives under strong magnetic fields, however, remains an open question. The layered honeycomb magnet $α$-RuCl$_3$ is a leading candidate material: an in-plane field of $\sim$ 7 T suppresses antiferromagnetic order and induces a quantum-disordered phase exhibiting signatures consistent with Majorana excitations, including an anomalous thermal Hall effect and field-angle-dependent specific heat. At higher fields, the magnetization approaches saturation, suggesting a transition to a spin-polarized state, yet the microscopic evolution between these limits remains unresolved. Here we report high-field specific heat measurements up to 24 T that reveal a distinct crossover at $μ_0H^*\approx$15 T, beyond which the perturbative Kitaev description breaks down. Above $H^*$, the characteristic six-fold angular modulation of the specific heat collapses and the excitation gap deviates from the predicted $H^3$ scaling. Meanwhile, the gap decreases with increasing field and the in-plane magnetization anisotropy persists up to $\sim$ 24 T, both in sharp contrast to a trivial spin-polarized state, indicating that KQSL signatures are preserved even at $\sim$ 90 % of magnetization saturation. These results reveal that the KQSL in $α$-RuCl$_3$ extends well beyond the perturbative window, persisting as a nonperturbative regime in which the Majorana and vison energy scales merge, before eventually giving way to spin polarization. This thermodynamic roadmap provides a basis for understanding how fractionalized phases evolve under strong magnetic fields.

</details>


### [144] [Hubb_DMFT and Wan2mb_DMFT: Continuous-Time Quantum Monte Carlo Solvers for Single- and Multi-Orbital Hubbard Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18540)
*A. A. Katanin*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文介绍了两套相互关联的自定义软件包 Hubb_DMFT 和 Wan2mb_DMFT，用于求解强关联电子系统中的动力学平均场理论（Dynamical Mean-Field Theory, DMFT）方程。前者针对单带 Hubbard 模型快速计算局域自能和二粒子费米子及三角费米子-玻色子电荷、自旋顶点，后者将能力扩展至真实多轨道系统，直接对接 Wannier 紧束缚哈密顿量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强关联电子系统的数值求解对研究材料性质至关重要，但现有工具在单带模型和真实多轨道系统之间的覆盖存在不足，需要开发兼具速度和通用性的 DMFT 求解软件。

Method: 两套代码均基于 iQIST v.0.7 杂质求解器，采用改进并内部集成的连续时间量子蒙特卡洛（CT-QMC）核心，基于杂化展开框架（CT-HYB），并针对密度-密度相互作用改进了自能和顶点估计器，以确保量子杂质问题的数值精确解。Hubb_DMFT 专用于单带 Hubbard 模型，Wan2mb_DMFT 通过接口将 Wannier 紧束缚哈密顿量与多体求解器结合。

Result: Hubb_DMFT 可高效计算局域自能（self-energy）、二粒子费米子顶点以及三角费米子-玻色子电荷和自旋顶点；Wan2mb_DMFT 实现了对真实多轨道系统的 DMFT 求解，直接使用 Wannier 紧束缚哈密顿量作为输入。

Conclusion: 两套软件包为强关联电子系统的 DMFT 计算提供了从模型到材料的完整求解方案，兼顾数值精度与计算效率。

Abstract: We present two related customized software packages, Hubb_DMFT and Wan2mb_DMFT, designed to solve the Dynamical Mean-Field Theory (DMFT) equations for strongly correlated electron systems. Hubb_DMFT is adjusted for the single-band Hubbard model, providing a fast way to calculate the local self-energy, as well as the two-particle fermion and triangular fermion-boson charge and spin vertices, while Wan2mb_DMFT extends this capability to realistic multi-orbital systems, directly interfacing Wannier tight-binding Hamiltonians with many-body solvers. Both codes are based on iQIST v.0.7 impurity solver and utilize a modified and internally integrated Continuous-Time Quantum Monte Carlo (CT-QMC) core with the hybridization expansion (CT-HYB) framework and improved self-energy and vertex estimators for density-density interaction, ensuring numerically exact solutions for quantum impurity problems.

</details>


### [145] [Transitions and Critical Divergences in Periodically Hopping Modulated Su-Schrieffer-Heeger Chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18706)
*Surajit Mandal,Satyaki Kar*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文采用曲率重整化群（curvature renormalization group, CRG）方法研究Su-Schrieffer-Heeger（SSH）链及其周期调制扩展模型中的拓扑相变，分析了曲率函数的发散行为、临界指数及边界态衰减特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨拓扑相变现象是否需要一种类似于传统相变中重整化群方法的统一理论框架。作者试图将曲率函数在临界点的发散行为与传统相变（如磁相变）中的有序参量涨落相关联，以建立拓扑相变的临界标度理论。

Method: 定义以高对称点附近系统参数为变量的曲率函数（curvature function），曲率函数在临界点处的发散被用来标志拓扑相变的发生。该方法应用于三种模型：两格点SSH模型、跳跃调制周期为4个晶格间距的SSH模型，以及跳跃调制周期为8个晶格间距的SSH模型。

Result: 两格点SSH模型的相变线位于Δ=0处，此处曲率函数发散。周期为4的调制模型在拓扑相变点|Δ/t|=√2处出现类狄拉克（Dirac-like）发散，同时也在非拓扑无隙点Δ=0处表现出更快的发散。相关长度在Δ→0方向的发散速度快于|Δ/t|→√2方向，导致两套不同的临界指数，对应不同的普适类（universality class）。Δ→0附近边界态向体态衰减极慢，而|Δ/t|→√2附近衰减明显更快。

Conclusion: CRG方法能够有效刻画不同SSH模型的拓扑相变，且揭示了不同临界点可能属于不同普适类、具有不同临界行为这一重要物理现象。对周期为8的SSH模型的类似分析也在论文中展开。

Abstract: We use a curvature renormalization group (CRG) approach to study the topological phase transitions in a Su-Schrieffer-Heeger chain and its extensions coming from periodic hopping modulations. A curvature function is defined in terms of system parameters near high-symmetry points where the divergence of this function at critical points, in analogy to usual phase transitions, signals a topological phase transition. According to this theory, the phase transition line for the two-site Su-Schrieffer-Heeger (SSH) model is visible at the critical line Δ= 0 where the curvature function diverges. Our study involves this model and also the modulated one with periodicity of four lattice spacing where the curvature function not only diverges at the topological phase transition point (Dirac-like) |Δ/t| = \sqrt(2) but also shows faster divergence at the non-topological gapless point Δ= 0. We further notice faster divergence of correlation length for Δ-> 0 as compared to that for the |Δ/t| -> \sqrt(2) resulting in two different sets of critical exponents making them lie in different universality classes. The edge state exhibits very slow decay into the bulk near the Δ= 0 point while a much quicker decay from edge into bulk is discernible around the |Δ/t| = \sqrt(2) point. We also continue similar analysis for a SSH model with hopping periodicity of eight lattice spacing.

</details>


### [146] [Sign-problem-resilient singular-value probe in determinant quantum Monte Carlo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19028)
*Wen Chen,Rubem Mondaini*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出利用时空费米子矩阵低阶奇异值的相邻能隙比作为探针，在行列式量子蒙特卡洛（determinant quantum Monte Carlo）中检测强关联费米子体系的相互作用驱动相变，且该探针在符号问题（sign problem）存在时依然有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 符号问题限制了行列式量子蒙特卡洛对强关联费米子系统研究的深度，因此亟需一种对符号问题具有鲁棒性的相变探针。

Method: 利用自旋通道Hubbard-Stratonovich分解中自旋关联与辅助场关联的精确对应关系，通过计算时空费米子矩阵低阶奇异值的相邻能隙比，在两个半填充蜂巢格Hubbard模型（无符号的蜂巢Hubbard模型和复权重的Haldane-Hubbard模型）中检测相互作用驱动的相变。

Result: 在无符号蜂巢Hubbard模型中，该统计量准确追踪了从狄拉克半金属到反铁磁莫特绝缘体的已知相变；在复权重的Haldane-Hubbard模型中，该特征在相位淬火参考系中依然可见，其位置与先前估计的相变点接近；尽管平均相位快速抑制，相位重加权仅对能隙比产生微弱修正。

Conclusion: 奇异值统计可作为对符号问题具有鲁棒性的探针，在行列式量子蒙特卡洛中检测相互作用驱动的相变。

Abstract: The sign problem limits determinant quantum Monte Carlo studies of strongly correlated fermion systems. In the spin-channel Hubbard-Stratonovich decoupling, spin correlations are exactly related to auxiliary-field correlations. This relation implies that an antiferromagnetic transition reorganizes auxiliary-field configurations and thereby changes the statistical structure of the resulting fermion matrices. We use the adjacent gap ratio of low-lying singular values of the space-time fermion matrix to probe interaction-driven transitions in two half-filled honeycomb-lattice Hubbard models. In the sign-free honeycomb Hubbard model, the statistic tracks the established transition from a Dirac semimetal to an antiferromagnetic Mott insulator. In the complex-weight Haldane-Hubbard model, the transition-sensitive feature remains visible in the phase-quenched reference ensemble and occurs near previous estimates of the transition. Moreover, phase reweighting only weakly modifies the gap ratio over the regimes investigated, despite the rapid suppression of the average phase. These results establish singular-value statistics as a sign-problem-resilient probe of interaction-driven transitions in determinant quantum Monte Carlo.

</details>


### [147] [Chiral bosonic mean-field Ansatz and spin dynamics in spin-1 Kitaev magnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19178)
*Daiki Sasamoto,Arnaud Ralko,Jaime Merino,Joji Nasu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文基于Schwinger玻色子平均场理论中的键算符表述（bond-operator formulation），重新审视反铁磁S=1 Kitaev模型中三重态π/2通量（π/2-flux）相图式，并扩展到弱对称非对角交换Γ与Γ'，通过计算动力学自旋结构因子与实空间自旋关联，论证三重态π/2-通量手性玻色子Ansatz可作为近纯Kitaev极限下自旋动力学的有效平均场描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kitaev模型是量子自旋液体（quantum spin liquid）的范示例，但其高自旋扩展不可精确求解，且其自旋动力学相较于自旋1/2情形研究不足，因此需要发展有效的平均场方法来描述S=1 Kitaev模型的自旋动力学。

Method: 采用Schwinger-玻色子平均场理论（Schwinger-boson mean-field theory）的键算符表述，对三重态0-通量和π/2-通量Ansätze计算动力学自旋结构因子S(q,ω)，并使用适用于Kitaev相互作用的自旋关联方案计算实空间自旋关联；同时沿Γ'=Γ方向研究弱对称非对角交换。

Result: 在纯Kitaev极限下，π/2-通量Ansatz给出比0-通量更平坦的谱；π/2-通量Ansatz比0-通量更强烈地抑制长距离自旋关联，产生更接近Kitaev极限预期的短程关联模式；在弱非对角交换下，约束的π/2-通量流形内获得有能隙解，其谱与有限尺寸精确对角化结果定性一致。

Conclusion: S(q,ω)的平坦性与短程自旋关联相关，可作为重要的一致性检验，但本身不能诊断时间反演对称性破缺（time-reversal-symmetry breaking）；三重态π/2-通量手性玻色子Ansatz是近反铁磁S=1 Kitaev极限含弱非对角交换情形下自旋动力学的有用平均场描述。

Abstract: The Kitaev model is a paradigmatic system for realizing quantum spin liquids, but its higher-spin extensions are not exactly solvable, and their spin dynamics is less well understood than in the spin-1/2 case. In this work, we reexamine a previously introduced triplet-pairing $φ_t = π/2$ phase pattern for the antiferromagnetic $S = 1$ Kitaev model and extend the analysis to weak symmetric off-diagonal exchanges $Γ$ and $Γ'$. Using a bond-operator formulation of Schwinger-boson mean-field theory, we calculate the dynamical spin structure factor for the triplet 0-flux and triplet $π/2$-flux Ansätze with a spin-correlation scheme appropriate for Kitaev interactions. In the pure Kitaev limit, the $π/2$-flux Ansatz yields a flatter spectrum than the 0-flux Ansatz. The real-space spin correlations show that the $π/2$-flux Ansatz suppresses longer-distance correlations more strongly than the 0-flux Ansatz, yielding a correlation pattern closer to the short-ranged form expected in the Kitaev limit. This comparison shows that the flatness of $S(\boldsymbol{q}, ω)$ is tied to short-ranged spin correlations and is therefore an important consistency check, although it is not, by itself, a diagnostic of time-reversal-symmetry breaking. We then study weak off-diagonal exchanges along $Γ' = Γ$ near the pure Kitaev limit, taking the same-sign relation from analyses of candidate spin-1 Kitaev materials. Gapped solutions are obtained within the constrained $π/2$-flux manifold, and the spectra share the qualitative energy- and momentum-space features found by finite-size exact diagonalization. Taken together, these results support the triplet $π/2$-flux chiral bosonic Ansatz as a useful mean-field description of spin dynamics near the antiferromagnetic $S = 1$ Kitaev limit with weak off-diagonal exchanges.

</details>


### [148] [Unconventional bond- and current-density waves on hexagonal lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19185)
*Andras Szabo,Hannes Braun,Laura Classen*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个描述非常规电荷密度波（CDW）相的统一框架，该框架同时整合了由小群（little group）描述的局域对称性和多个对称相关有序波矢（星）的多分量序参量变换性质，并应用于六重对称二维晶格及三角晶格微观模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统CDW序被描述为有序波矢Q处局域电荷的调制，但近期研究发现键序和环流密度波等非常规相在晶胞内具有非平凡纹理，源于非零角动量的粒子—空穴凝聚体，需要一种同时考虑小群对称性和多波矢星结构的系统性理论框架来描述这些相的对称性和序参量变换规律。

Method: 该框架将多分量序参量视为由小群G_Q的不可约表示诱导的全空间群表示，从而同时纳入局域对称性与多个对称相关的有序波矢；在此基础上导出对应的朗道自由能（Landau free energy），并利用随机相位近似（random phase approximation）在三角晶格上从电子相互作用出发推导非常规键序和环流序的涌现。

Result: 作者将该框架应用于具有六重对称性的二维晶格和沿高对称线的有序波矢，导出了相应的朗道自由能；在三角晶格上通过随机相位近似微观计算了自由能中的相关系数，并确定了非常规键序与环流序的基态。

Conclusion: 该框架为描述非常规调制相提供了一条系统化途径，且可便捷地推广到更复杂的晶格结构。

Abstract: Charge-density wave (CDW) orders are conventionally described as modulations of on-site charge at an ordering wave vector $\boldsymbol{Q}$ with symmetry-related wave vectors typically forming a multicomponent order-parameter manifold. Recent developments significantly broadened this phenomenology to bond and loop-current density waves, which possess nontrivial, potentially symmetry-breaking textures within the unit cell in addition to their spatial modulation at $\boldsymbol{Q}$. Such textures arise from particle-hole condensates with nonzero angular momentum, analogous to unconventional superconductivity, and their symmetries are described by the little group $G_{\boldsymbol{Q}}$. In this work, we develop a framework for unconventional CDW phases that simultaneously incorporates the local symmetries described by little group and the presence of multiple symmetry-related ordering wave vectors, also known as the star. The resulting multicomponent order parameter transforms under representations of the full space group induced from irreducible representations of $G_{\boldsymbol{Q}}$. We apply this framework to two-dimensional lattices with sixfold symmetry and ordering wave vectors along high-symmetry lines, and derive the corresponding Landau free energies. As a microscopic example, we demonstrate the emergence of unconventional bond and loop-current orders from electronic interactions on the triangular lattice within the random phase approximation, and determine their ground states by microscopically evaluating the relevant coefficients in the free energy. Our framework provides a systematic route to describing unconventional modulated phases and can be readily extended to more complex lattices.

</details>
